<div class="col-sm order-1">
      <h2 class="chapter_title">CHAPTER 326. EMPLOYMENTS LICENSED BY STATE</h2>
      
      <div class="section" id="stat.326.01">
        <h1 class="shn">326.01 DEFINITIONS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.01.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.01.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Words, terms and phrases.</span></h2>

          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 11.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/140/#laws.13.3.0">2007 c 140 art 13 s 3</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.01.12">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 12." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.01.12">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 12.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/140/#laws.13.3.0">2007 c 140 art 13 s 3</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.01.13">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 13." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.01.13">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 13.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/140/#laws.13.3.0">2007 c 140 art 13 s 3</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.01.14">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 14." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.01.14">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 14.<span class="headnote">Person.</span></h2>
          <p>The term "person" includes an individual, partnership, association, joint stock company, trust, or corporation.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.01.15">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 15." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.01.15">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 15.<span class="headnote">Distributor.</span></h2>
          <p>The term "distributor" includes any person who engages, or contracts to engage, in the distribution of motion picture films and is a resident of, or legally authorized to do business in, this state.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.01.16">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 16." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.01.16">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 16.<span class="headnote">Exhibitor.</span></h2>
          <p>The term "exhibitor" includes any person who engages, or contracts to engage, in the exhibition of motion picture films and is a resident of, or legally authorized to do business in, this state.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.01.17">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 17." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.01.17">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 17.<span class="headnote">License.</span></h2>
          <p>The term "license" includes the offering, intending or making of a license agreement, contract, or any type of agreement whereby a film, the distribution of which is controlled by one of the parties is to be supplied to and exhibited in a theatre owned, controlled, or operated by the other party.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.01.18">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 18." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.01.18">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 18.<span class="headnote">Feature motion picture film.</span></h2>
          <p>The term "feature motion picture film" means all motion pictures, whether copyrighted or uncopyrighted, including positive and negative prints and copies or reproductions of such prints, which films contain photoplays or other subjects and are produced for public exhibition. The term shall not include films commonly known as short subjects, newsreels, trailers, serials, reissues, foreign, and western pictures, and road shows.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.01.19">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 19." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.01.19">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 19.<span class="headnote">Exhibition season.</span></h2>
          <p>The term "exhibition season" means a period of 12 months as may be selected by the producer-distributor, but there shall be no lapse of time between the termination of one season and the beginning of the next.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.01.20">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 20." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.01.20">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 20.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1992/0/464/#laws.1.19.0">1992 c 464 art 1 s 19</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.01.21">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 21." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.01.21">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 21.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/209/#laws.1.33.0">1989 c 209 art 1 s 33</a>]</p>
        </div>
      </div>

      <h1 class="fgh">ARCHITECTS, ENGINEERS, SURVEYORS,<br>LANDSCAPE ARCHITECTS, GEOSCIENTISTS,<br>INTERIOR DESIGNERS</h1>
      <div class="section" id="stat.326.02">
        <h1 class="shn">326.02 LICENSURE OR CERTIFICATION.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.02.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.02.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Licensure or certification mandatory.</span></h2>
          <p>In order to safeguard life, health, and property, and to promote the public welfare, any person in either public or private capacity practicing, or offering to practice, architecture, professional engineering, land surveying, landscape architecture, or professional geoscience, or using the title certified interior designer in this state, either as an individual, a copartner, or as agent of another, shall be licensed or certified as hereinafter provided. It shall be unlawful for any person to practice, or to offer to practice, in this state, architecture, professional engineering, land surveying, landscape architecture, or professional geoscience, or to use the title certified interior designer, or to solicit or to contract to furnish work within the terms of sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.02">326.02</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.15">326.15</a>, or to use in connection with the person's name, or to otherwise assume, use or advertise any title or description tending to convey the impression that the person is an architect, professional engineer (hereinafter called engineer), land surveyor, landscape architect, professional geoscientist (hereinafter called geoscientist), or certified interior designer, unless such person is qualified by licensure or certification under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.02">326.02</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.15">326.15</a>. This subdivision does not preclude an individual who retired from one of the professions listed in this subdivision from using the designation architect, professional engineer, land surveyor, landscape architect, professional geoscientist, or certified interior designer as long as the designation is preceded by the word "retired" and the individual was licensed or certified in the designated profession in the state of Minnesota on the date the individual retired from the designated profession and the individual's license or certification was not subsequently revoked by the Board of Architecture, Engineering, Land Surveying, Landscape Architecture, Geoscience, and Interior Design.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.02.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.02.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Practice of architecture.</span></h2>
          <p>Any person shall be deemed to be practicing architecture, within the meaning of sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.02">326.02</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.15">326.15</a>, who holds out as being able to perform or who does perform any professional service, such as planning, design, or supervision of construction for the purpose of assuring compliance with specifications and design, in connection with any private or public buildings, structures or projects, or the equipment or utilities thereof, or the accessories thereto, wherein the safeguarding of life, health, or property is concerned or involved, when such professional service requires the application of the art and science of construction based upon the principles of mathematics, aesthetics, and the physical sciences, acquired by education or training, and by experience. For the purposes of this subdivision "supervision" is a professional service as distinguished from superintending of construction and means the performance or the supervision thereof, of reasonable and ordinary on the site observations to determine that the construction is in substantial compliance with the approved drawings, plans and specifications.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.02.3">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.02.3">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Practice of professional engineering.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Any person shall be deemed to be practicing professional engineering within the meaning of sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.02">326.02</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.15">326.15 </a>who holds out as being able to perform or who does perform any technical professional service, such as planning, design or observation of construction for the purpose of assuring compliance with specifications and design, in connection with any public or private structures, buildings, utilities, machines, equipment, processes, works, or projects wherein the public welfare or the safeguarding of life, health, or property is concerned or involved, when such professional service requires the application of the principles of mathematics and the physical and applied engineering sciences, acquired by education or training, and by experience.</p>
          <p>(b) No person other than one licensed under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.02">326.02</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.15">326.15</a> as a professional engineer may:</p>
          <p>(1) use the term "professional engineer";</p>
          <p>(2) use any other abbreviation or term, including the initials "P.E." or "PE" by signature, verbal claim, sign, advertisement, letterhead, card, or similar means that would lead the public to believe that the person was a professional engineer; or</p>
          <p>(3) use any means or in any other way make a representation that would lead the public to believe that the person was a professional engineer.</p>
          <p>(c) A professional engineering license satisfies any requirements by the state or its political subdivisions to perform any actions authorized under the professional engineering license. A person licensed as a professional engineer under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.02">326.02</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.15">326.15</a> shall only be required to obtain a license, certification, or other form of approval for a skill or service in addition to a professional engineering license if the state or political subdivision has made an affirmative written determination in statute, rule, or ordinance that such additional license or certification is necessary to safeguard life, health, or property, or promote the public welfare. This section does not preclude the state or a political subdivision from including additional requirements when soliciting public contracts for engineering services. This paragraph does not apply to practice areas where licenses or certifications are required prior to August 1, 2015.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.02.3a">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3a." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.02.3a">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3a.<span class="headnote">Practice of professional geoscience.</span></h2>
          <p>A person is considered to be practicing professional geoscience within the meaning of sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.02">326.02</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.15">326.15</a> who holds out as being able to perform or who does perform any technical professional services, the adequate performance of which requires professional geoscience education, training, and experience in the application of special knowledge of the mathematical, physical, chemical, biological, and earth sciences to such services or creative work as consultation, investigation, evaluation, planning, mapping, and inspection of geoscientific work and its responsible supervision.</p>
          <p>A person is considered to practice or offer to practice professional geoscience, within the meaning and intent of sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.02">326.02</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.15">326.15</a> who practices any of the geoscience disciplines defined by the board; who by verbal claim, sign, advertisement, letterhead, card, or in any other way represents oneself to be a professional geoscientist; through the use of some other title implies that the person is a professional geoscientist; or who presents oneself as able to perform or who does perform any geoscience services or that constitutes the practice of a professional geoscience discipline as defined by the board.</p>
          <p>"Geoscience" means the science which includes treatment of the earth and its origin and history; the investigation, measurement or sampling, of the earth's constituent rocks, natural and induced fields of force, minerals, fossils, solids, soils, fluids including surface and underground waters, gases, and other materials; and the study, interpretation, and analysis of the natural agents, forces, and processes which cause changes in the earth.</p>
          <p>Nothing in this subdivision shall be construed to prevent a professional engineer, as defined in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.02">326.02</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.15">326.15</a>, from acquiring engineering data involving soil, rock, groundwater, and other earth materials; evaluating physical and chemical properties of soil, rock, groundwater, and other earth materials for engineering; and from utilizing these data for analysis, design, and construction. Nothing in this subdivision shall be construed to permit a professional geoscientist to engage in the practice of professional engineering, architecture, landscape architecture, or land surveying or to use the title "certified interior design" as those terms are defined in this section. Nothing in this subdivision shall be construed to regulate persons who take soil samples for the purpose of providing recommendations on crop production.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.02.4">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.02.4">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Practice of land surveying.</span></h2>
          <p>Land surveying means the application of the principles of mathematics, physical and applied sciences and law to measuring and locating lines, angles, elevations and natural or artificial features in the air, on the surface of the earth, underground and on the beds of bodies of water for the purpose of:</p>
          <p>(1) determining, monumenting, establishing, or reestablishing property boundaries;</p>
          <p>(2) determining, monumenting, or reestablishing the position for any public land survey system corner or line;</p>
          <p>(3) planning, designing, and platting of land and subdivisions including the topography, alignment and grades of streets; and</p>
          <p>(4) preparing and perpetuating maps, record plats, and property descriptions.</p>
          <p>Any person who offers to perform, holds out as being able to perform, or does perform land surveying for others shall be practicing land surveying.</p>
          <p>Nothing contained in the provisions of sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.02">326.02</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.15">326.15</a>, shall prohibit a licensed professional engineer, architect, landscape architect, or professional geoscientist from doing any work included in the practice of engineering, architecture, landscape architecture, and professional geoscience, if the work does not involve the establishment or reestablishment of property corners, property lines, or public land survey system corners or lines.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.02.4a">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4a." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.02.4a">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4a.<span class="headnote">Practice of landscape architecture.</span></h2>
          <p>Any person shall be deemed to be practicing landscape architecture, within the meaning of sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.02">326.02</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.15">326.15</a>, who holds out as being able to perform or who does perform any professional service in connection with the development of land areas where the dominant purpose of the service is the preservation, enhancement or determination of proper land uses, natural land features, ground cover and planting, naturalistic and aesthetic values, the settings, approaches or environment for structures or other improvements, and the consideration and determination of inherent problems of the land relating to erosion, wear and tear, blight and hazards. This practice shall include the location and arrangement of tangible objects and features incidental and necessary to the purposes outlined but shall not include the design of structures or facilities with separate and self-contained purposes as ordinarily included in the practice of engineering or architecture or the preparation of boundary surveys or final land plats, as ordinarily included in the practice of land surveying.</p>
          <p>Nothing contained in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.02">326.02</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.15">326.15</a> concerning landscape architects shall be construed:</p>
          <p>(1) to apply to a professional engineer duly registered under the laws of this state;</p>
          <p>(2) to apply to an architect registered under the laws of this state;</p>
          <p>(3) to apply to a land surveyor registered under the laws of this state;</p>
          <p>(4) to prevent a registered architect or professional engineer from doing landscape planning and designing;</p>
          <p>(5) to exclude nursery operators or other small business people from the preparation of landscape plans appropriate to the normal operation of their business;</p>
          <p>(6) to authorize a landscape architect to engage in the practice of architecture, engineering, land surveying, or geoscience.</p>
          <p>No person shall use the designation landscape architect or any title or device indicating or representing that the person is a landscape architect or is practicing landscape architecture unless the person is registered under the provisions of sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.02">326.02</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.15">326.15</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.02.4b">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4b." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.02.4b">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4b.<span class="headnote">Certified interior designer.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) For the purposes of sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.02">326.02</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.15">326.15</a>, "certified interior designer" means a person who is certified under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.10">326.10</a>, to use the title certified interior designer and who provides services in connection with the design of public interior spaces, including preparation of documents relative to non-load-bearing interior construction, space planning, finish materials, and furnishings.</p>
          <p>(b) No person may use the title certified interior designer unless that person has been certified as an interior designer or has been exempted by the board. Registered architects may be certified without additional testing. Persons represent themselves to the public as certified interior designers if they use a title that incorporates the words certified interior designer.</p>
          <p>(c) Nothing in this section prohibits the use of the title interior designer or the term interior design by persons not certified by the board.</p>
          <p>(d) Nothing in this section restricts persons not certified by the board from providing interior design services and from saying that they provide such services, as long as they do not use the title certified interior designer.</p>
          <p>(e) Nothing in this section authorizes certified interior designers to engage in the practice of architecture as defined in subdivision 2 or the practice of engineering as defined in subdivision 3.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.02.5">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 5." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.02.5">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Limitation.</span></h2>
          <p>The provisions of sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.02">326.02</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.15">326.15</a> shall not apply to the preparation of plans and specifications for the erection, enlargement, or alteration of any building or other structure by any person, for that person's exclusive occupancy or use, unless such occupancy or use involves the public health or safety or the health or safety of the employees of said person, or of the buildings listed in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.03#stat.326.03.2">326.03, subdivision 2</a>, nor to any detailed or shop plans required to be furnished by a contractor to a registered engineer, landscape architect, architect, or certified interior designer, nor to any standardized manufactured product, nor to any construction superintendent supervising the execution of work designed by an architect, landscape architect, engineer, or certified interior designer licensed or certified in accordance with section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.03">326.03</a>, nor to the planning for and supervision of the construction and installation of work by an electrical or elevator contractor or master plumber as defined in and licensed pursuant to chapter 326B, nor to the planning for and supervision of the construction and installation of work by a licensed well contractor as defined and licensed pursuant to chapter 103I, where such work is within the scope of such licensed activity and not within the practice of professional engineering, or architecture, or where the person does not claim to be a certified interior designer as defined in subdivision 2, 3, or 4b.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.326.02.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">(<a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1940A/cite/35">5697-1</a>) <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1921/0/523/#laws.0.1.0">1921 c 523 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1933/0/404/#laws.0.1.0">1933 c 404 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1945/0/380/#laws.0.1.0">1945 c 380 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1967/1/28/#laws.0.1.0">Ex1967 c 28 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1971/0/22/#laws.0.1.0">1971 c 22 s 1</a>-3; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1973/0/245/#laws.0.1.0">1973 c 245 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1975/0/329/#laws.0.1.0">1975 c 329 s 1</a>-3; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1979/0/209/#laws.0.1.0">1979 c 209 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1981/1/4/#laws.1.25.0">1Sp1981 c 4 art 1 s 25</a>-29; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/0/444/#laws.0.0.0">1986 c 444</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1992/0/507/#laws.0.3.0">1992 c 507 s 3</a>-5; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/0/206/#laws.0.4.0">1995 c 206 s 4</a>-7; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2002/0/239/#laws.0.1.0">2002 c 239 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2010/0/280/#laws.0.8.0">2010 c 280 s 8</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2013/0/85/#laws.2.3.0">2013 c 85 art 2 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/236/#laws.0.1.0">2014 c 236 s 1</a>,2; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2015/0/18/#laws.0.1.0">2015 c 18 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/110/#laws.1.1.0">2024 c 110 art 1 s 1</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.326.03">
        <h1 class="shn">326.03 LICENSE OR CERTIFICATE REQUIRED.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.03.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.03.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Plans; documents.</span></h2>
          <p>No person, except an architect, engineer, land surveyor, landscape architect, geoscientist, or certified interior designer, licensed or certified as provided for in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.02">326.02</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.15">326.15</a> shall practice architecture, professional engineering, land surveying, landscape architecture, or professional geoscience, or use the title certified interior designer, respectively, in the preparation of plans, specifications, reports, plats or other architectural, engineering, land surveying, landscape architectural, geoscientific, or interior design documents, or in the observation of architectural, engineering, land surveying, landscape architectural, geoscientific, or interior design projects. In preparation of such documents, reasonable care shall be given to compliance with applicable laws, ordinances, and building codes relating to design.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.03.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.03.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Exceptions.</span></h2>
          <p>Nothing contained in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.02">326.02</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.15">326.15</a> shall prevent persons from advertising and performing services such as consultation, investigation, or evaluation in connection with, or from making plans and specifications for, or from supervising, the erection, enlargement, or alteration of any of the following buildings:</p>
          <p>(1) dwellings for single families, and outbuildings in connection therewith, such as barns and private garages;</p>
          <p>(2) two family dwellings;</p>
          <p>(3) any farm building or accessory thereto; or</p>
          <p>(4) temporary buildings or sheds used exclusively for construction purposes, not exceeding two stories in height, and not used for living quarters.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.03.3">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.03.3">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Recording.</span></h2>
          <p>No plat, map, or drawing of any survey or subdivision of lands required by law to be filed or recorded with the county recorder or registered with the registrar of titles of any county, shall be filed, recorded, or registered therein unless there shall be endorsed thereon a certification by a licensed land surveyor.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.03.4">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.03.4">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Exception for persons elected in office.</span></h2>
          <p>The provisions hereof shall not apply to any person holding an elective office when in discharging the duties thereof such person is required to do work or perform service of the character of work or service usually done or performed by an architect, engineer, land surveyor, landscape architect, or geoscientist.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.03.5">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 5." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.03.5">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Insurance companies; rating bureaus.</span></h2>
          <p>The provisions of sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.02">326.02</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.15">326.15</a> shall not apply to inspection and service work done by employees of insurance companies, their agents, or insurance rating bureaus.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.326.03.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">(<a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1940A/cite/35">5697-2</a>) <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1921/0/523/#laws.0.2.0">1921 c 523 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1933/0/404/#laws.0.1.0">1933 c 404 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1945/0/380/#laws.0.2.0">1945 c 380 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1967/1/28/#laws.0.2.0">Ex1967 c 28 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1975/0/83/#laws.0.1.0">1975 c 83 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1975/0/329/#laws.0.4.0">1975 c 329 s 4</a>,5; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1976/0/181/#laws.0.2.0">1976 c 181 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1976/0/222/#laws.0.138.0">1976 c 222 s 138</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1978/0/577/#laws.0.1.0">1978 c 577 s 1</a>,2; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1981/1/4/#laws.1.30.0">1Sp1981 c 4 art 1 s 30</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/8/#laws.0.1.0">1987 c 8 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/384/#laws.1.30.0">1987 c 384 art 1 s 30</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/329/#laws.5.15.0">1989 c 329 art 5 s 15</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1990/0/562/#laws.5.11.0">1990 c 562 art 5 s 11</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1992/0/507/#laws.0.6.0">1992 c 507 s 6</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/0/206/#laws.0.8.0">1995 c 206 s 8</a>,9; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1998/0/324/#laws.0.9.0">1998 c 324 s 9</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.326.031">
        <h1 class="shn">326.031 SPECIFICATIONS FOR PUBLIC FACILITIES, USE OF BRAND NAMES.</h1>
        <p>Any engineer, architect, certified interior designer, or other person preparing specifications with respect to a contract for the construction of any facility for the state, or any agency or department thereof, or for any county, city, town, or school district, shall at the time of submitting such specifications to the governing body of the organization requesting the specifications, submit to such body, in writing, a list showing each item in the specifications which has been specified by brand name, unless such specifications allow for the consideration of an equal.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.326.031.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1969/0/635/#laws.0.1.0">1969 c 635 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1973/0/123/#laws.5.7.0">1973 c 123 art 5 s 7</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1992/0/507/#laws.0.7.0">1992 c 507 s 7</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.326.04">
        <h1 class="shn">326.04 BOARD ESTABLISHED.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.04.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.04.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Board composition.</span></h2>
          <p>To carry out the provisions of sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.02">326.02 </a>to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.15">326.15</a> there is hereby created a Board of Architecture, Engineering, Land Surveying, Landscape Architecture, Geoscience, and Interior Design consisting of 21 members, who shall be appointed by the governor. Three members shall be licensed architects, five members shall be licensed engineers, two members shall be licensed landscape architects, two members shall be licensed land surveyors, two members shall be certified interior designers, two members shall be licensed geoscientists, and five members shall be public members. Not more than one member of the board shall be from the same branch of the profession of engineering. Membership terms, compensation of members, removal of members, the filling of membership vacancies, and fiscal year and reporting requirements shall be as provided in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/214.07">214.07</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/214.09">214.09</a>. Members shall be limited to two terms. The provision of staff, administrative services and office space; the review and processing of complaints; the setting of board fees; and other provisions relating to board operations shall be as provided in chapter 214.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.04.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.04.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Temporary military certificate.</span></h2>
          <p>The board shall establish a temporary military certificate in accordance with section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/197.4552">197.4552</a>.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.326.04.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">(<a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1940A/cite/35">5697-3</a>) <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1921/0/523/#laws.0.3.0">1921 c 523 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1949/0/86/#laws.0.1.0">1949 c 86 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1973/0/638/#laws.0.42.0">1973 c 638 s 42</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1975/0/136/#laws.0.55.0">1975 c 136 s 55</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1975/0/329/#laws.0.6.0">1975 c 329 s 6</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1976/0/222/#laws.0.139.0">1976 c 222 s 139</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1976/0/239/#laws.0.63.0">1976 c 239 s 63</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1979/0/209/#laws.0.2.0">1979 c 209 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1991/0/199/#laws.1.50.0">1991 c 199 art 1 s 50</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1992/0/507/#laws.0.8.0">1992 c 507 s 8</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/0/206/#laws.0.10.0">1995 c 206 s 10</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1998/0/324/#laws.0.1.0">1998 c 324 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/236/#laws.0.3.0">2014 c 236 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/312/#laws.4.19.0">2014 c 312 art 4 s 19</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.326.05">
        <h1 class="shn">326.05 QUALIFICATIONS OF BOARD MEMBERS.</h1>
        <p>Each member of the board shall be a resident of this state at the time of and throughout the member's appointment. Each member except the public members shall have been engaged in the practice of the relevant profession for at least ten years and shall have been in responsible charge of professional work requiring licensure as an architect, engineer, land surveyor, landscape architect, or geoscientist, or certification as an interior designer for at least five years.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.326.05.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">(<a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1940A/cite/35">5697-4</a>) <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1921/0/523/#laws.0.4.0">1921 c 523 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1973/0/638/#laws.0.43.0">1973 c 638 s 43</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1975/0/329/#laws.0.7.0">1975 c 329 s 7</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1976/0/222/#laws.0.140.0">1976 c 222 s 140</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/0/444/#laws.0.0.0">1986 c 444</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1992/0/507/#laws.0.9.0">1992 c 507 s 9</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/0/206/#laws.0.11.0">1995 c 206 s 11</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1998/0/324/#laws.0.2.0">1998 c 324 s 2</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.326.06">
        <h1 class="shn">326.06 GENERAL POWERS AND DUTIES OF BOARD.</h1>
        <p>Each member of the board shall receive a certificate of appointment from the governor, and, before beginning a term of office, shall file with the secretary of state the constitutional oath of office. The board shall adopt and have an official seal, which shall be affixed to all licenses granted; shall make all rules, not inconsistent with law, needed in performing its duties; and shall fix standards for determining the qualifications of applicants for certificates, which shall not exceed the requirements contained in the curriculum of a recognized school of architecture, landscape architecture, engineering, geoscience, or interior design. The board shall make rules to define classes of buildings with respect to which persons performing services described in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.03#stat.326.03.2">326.03, subdivision 2</a>, may be exempted from the provisions of sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.02">326.02</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.15">326.15</a>, by a finding of no probable risk to life, health, property or public welfare.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.326.06.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">(<a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1940A/cite/35">5697-5</a>) <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1921/0/523/#laws.0.5.0">1921 c 523 s 5</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1975/0/329/#laws.0.8.0">1975 c 329 s 8</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1976/0/222/#laws.0.141.0">1976 c 222 s 141</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1978/0/577/#laws.0.3.0">1978 c 577 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1979/0/222/#laws.0.1.0">1979 c 222 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/0/444/#laws.0.0.0">1986 c 444</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/8/#laws.0.2.0">1987 c 8 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/384/#laws.1.31.0">1987 c 384 art 1 s 31</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1992/0/507/#laws.0.10.0">1992 c 507 s 10</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/0/206/#laws.0.12.0">1995 c 206 s 12</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.326.07">
        <h1 class="shn">326.07 BOARD, MEETINGS, OFFICERS, QUORUM.</h1>
        <p>The board shall hold meetings at such times the board shall specify. Notice of all meetings shall be given in such manner as the bylaws may provide as described in chapter 13D. The board shall elect annually from its members a chair, a vice-chair, a secretary and a treasurer. A quorum of the board shall consist of a majority of members of the board qualified to vote on the matter in question.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.326.07.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">(<a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1940A/cite/35">5697-6</a>) <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1921/0/523/#laws.0.6.0">1921 c 523 s 6</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1949/0/86/#laws.0.2.0">1949 c 86 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1973/0/638/#laws.0.44.0">1973 c 638 s 44</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1975/0/136/#laws.0.56.0">1975 c 136 s 56</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1975/0/329/#laws.0.9.0">1975 c 329 s 9</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/0/444/#laws.0.0.0">1986 c 444</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1992/0/507/#laws.0.11.0">1992 c 507 s 11</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/0/206/#laws.0.13.0">1995 c 206 s 13</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1998/0/324/#laws.0.3.0">1998 c 324 s 3</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.326.08"><b>326.08</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1998/0/324/#laws.0.10.0">1998 c 324 s 10</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.326.09">
        <h1 class="shn">326.09 RECORDS OF BOARD.</h1>
        <p>The board shall keep a record of its proceedings and a register of all applicants for licensing, showing for each the date of application, name, age, educational and other qualifications, place of business, and the place of residence, whether or not an examination was required and whether the applicant was rejected or a license granted, and the date of such action. The books and register of the board shall be prima facie evidence of all matters recorded therein. A roster showing the names and places of business or of residence of all licensed architects, engineers, land surveyors, landscape architects, geoscientists, and certified interior designers shall be prepared annually. Rosters may be printed out of the funds of the board.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.326.09.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">(<a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1940A/cite/35">5697-8</a>) <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1921/0/523/#laws.0.8.0">1921 c 523 s 8</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1955/0/847/#laws.0.23.0">1955 c 847 s 23</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1957/0/15/#laws.0.0.0">1957 c 15</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1975/0/329/#laws.0.11.0">1975 c 329 s 11</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1976/0/222/#laws.0.144.0">1976 c 222 s 144</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1992/0/507/#laws.0.13.0">1992 c 507 s 13</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/0/206/#laws.0.15.0">1995 c 206 s 15</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1998/0/324/#laws.0.4.0">1998 c 324 s 4</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.326.10">
        <h1 class="shn">326.10 LICENSURE AND CERTIFICATION.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.10.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.10.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Issuance.</span></h2>
          <p> The board shall on application therefor on a prescribed form, and upon payment of a fee prescribed by rule of the board, issue a license or certificate as an architect, engineer, land surveyor, landscape architect, geoscientist, or certified interior designer. A separate fee shall be paid for each profession licensed.</p>
          <p>(1) To any person over 25 years of age, who is of good moral character and repute, and who has the experience and educational qualifications which the board by rule may prescribe.</p>
          <p>(2) To any person who holds an unexpired certificate of registration or license issued by proper authority in the District of Columbia, any state or territory of the United States, or any foreign country, in which the requirements for registration or licensure of architects, engineers, land surveyors, landscape architects, geoscientists, or certified interior designers, respectively, at the time of registration or licensure in the other jurisdiction, were equal, in the opinion of the board, to those fixed by the board and by the laws of this state, and in which similar privileges are extended to the holders of certificates of registration or licensure issued by this state. The board may require such person to submit a certificate of technical qualification from the National Council of Architectural Registration Boards in the case of an architect, from the National Council of Examiners for Engineering and Surveying in the case of an engineer, from the Council of Landscape Architectural Registration Boards in the case of a landscape architect, and from the National Council for Interior Design Qualification in the case of a certified interior designer.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.10.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.10.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Examination.</span></h2>
          <p>The board, or a committee of the board, may subject any applicant for licensure or certification to such examinations as may be deemed necessary to establish qualifications.</p>
          <p>In determining the qualifications of applicants, at least one member determining the qualifications must be licensed or certified in the same profession as that being evaluated.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.10.2a">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2a." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.10.2a">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2a.<span class="headnote">Needs of physically disabled, inclusion in examination.</span></h2>
          <p>Examinations for architect, civil structural engineer, landscape architect, and certified interior designer shall include questions which require the applicant to demonstrate knowledge of the design needs of people with physical disabilities and of the relevant statutes and codes.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.10.3">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.10.3">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1967/1/28/#laws.0.9.0">Ex1967 c 28 s 9</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.10.4">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.10.4">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1976/0/222/#laws.0.209.0">1976 c 222 s 209</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.10.5">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 5." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.10.5">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/0/85/#laws.0.5.0">2003 c 85 s 5</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.10.6">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 6." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.10.6">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1967/1/28/#laws.0.9.0">Ex1967 c 28 s 9</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.10.7">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 7." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.10.7">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Engineer, land surveyor, geoscientist; in-training.</span></h2>
          <p>(1) An applicant for certification as an engineer-in-training who is a graduate with a bachelor of engineering degree from a school or college having an accredited engineering curriculum or whose education, in the opinion of the board, is equivalent thereto, shall receive from the board, upon passing an examination in fundamental engineering subjects, a certificate stating that the applicant has passed such examination and that the applicant's name has been recorded as an engineer-in-training.</p>
          <p>(2) An applicant for certification as a land surveyor-in-training who is a graduate with a bachelor's degree from a school or college having an accredited engineering or land surveying curriculum or who has equivalent education, in the opinion of the board, shall receive from the board, upon passing a written examination in the fundamentals of mathematics and the basic principles of land surveying, a certificate stating that the applicant has passed such examination and that the applicant's name has been recorded as a land surveyor-in-training.</p>
          <p>(3) An applicant for certification as a geoscientist-in-training who is a graduate with a baccalaureate degree from a school or college having accreditation defined by the board and a geoscience or associated science curriculum approved by the board, shall receive from the board, upon passing the appropriate examination in fundamental geoscience subjects for the applicant's discipline as approved by the board, a certificate stating that the applicant's name has been recorded as a geoscientist-in-training with the appropriate geoscientist-in-training legend as approved by the board.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.10.8">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 8." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.10.8">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.<span class="headnote">Expiration and renewal.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) All licenses and certificates, other than in-training certificates, issued by the board expire at midnight on June 30 of each even-numbered calendar year if not renewed. A holder of a license or certificate issued by the board may renew it by completing and filing with the board an application for renewal consisting of a fully completed form provided by the board and the fee specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.105">326.105</a>. Both the fee and the application must be submitted at the same time and by June 30 of each even-numbered calendar year. The form must be signed by the applicant, contain all of the information requested, and clearly show that the licensee or certificate holder has completed the minimum number of required professional development hours or has been granted an exemption under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.107#stat.326.107.4">326.107, subdivision 4</a>. An application for renewal that does not comply with the requirements of this subdivision is an incomplete application and must not be accepted by the board.</p>
          <p>(b) No later than 30 days before the date a license or certificate expires, the board must send the license or certificate holder a notice by email that indicates the license or certificate is about to expire. The notice must include information on the process and requirements to renew the license or certificate. The application form for a new or renewed license or certificate issued by the board must request that the applicant provide an email address for the purpose of providing the notice under this paragraph. If the board does not possess a record of a license or certificate holder's email address, the board must send the notice to the holder by United States mail.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.10.9">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 9." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.10.9">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 9.<span class="headnote">Reinstatement of expired license or certificate.</span></h2>
          <p>A licensee or certificate holder whose license or certificate has expired may reinstate the expired license or certificate by satisfying all prior continuing education requirements to a maximum of 48 professional development hours, by paying all of the renewal fees due for all prior renewal periods that the license or certificate was expired and the current renewal period, and paying a delayed renewal fee in the amount set by the board. The continuing education requirement must be satisfied with professional development hours completed within the four years immediately prior to reinstatement and may not include any professional development hours that had previously been used to renew the license or certificate being reinstated.</p>
          <p>To reinstate an expired license or certificate, the licensee or certificate holder must file with the board an application for reinstatement consisting of a fully completed form provided by the board and the fees specified in this subdivision. The form must be signed, contain all of the information requested, and clearly show that the licensee or certificate holder either has completed the minimum number of required professional development hours or has been granted an exemption under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.107#stat.326.107.4">326.107, subdivision 4</a>. An application for reinstatement that does not comply with the requirements of this subdivision is an incomplete application and must not be accepted by the board.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.10.10">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 10." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.10.10">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 10.<span class="headnote">Temporary military license.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">The board shall establish a temporary license in accordance with section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/197.4552">197.4552</a> for the practice of architecture, professional engineering, geosciences, land surveying, landscape architecture, and interior design. The fee for the temporary license under this subdivision for the practice of architecture, professional engineering, geosciences, land surveying, landscape architecture, or interior design is $132.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.326.10.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">(<a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1940A/cite/35">5697-9</a>) <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1921/0/523/#laws.0.9.0">1921 c 523 s 9</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1933/0/404/#laws.0.2.0">1933 c 404 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1945/0/380/#laws.0.3.0">1945 c 380 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1949/0/86/#laws.0.3.0">1949 c 86 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1949/0/507/#laws.0.1.0">1949 c 507 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1955/0/433/#laws.0.1.0">1955 c 433 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1959/0/336/#laws.0.1.0">1959 c 336 s 1</a>-5; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1961/0/519/#laws.0.1.0">1961 c 519 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1975/0/329/#laws.0.12.0">1975 c 329 s 12</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1976/0/222/#laws.0.145.0">1976 c 222 s 145</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1978/0/483/#laws.0.1.0">1978 c 483 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/0/444/#laws.0.0.0">1986 c 444</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1992/0/507/#laws.0.14.0">1992 c 507 s 14</a>-16; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/0/206/#laws.0.16.0">1995 c 206 s 16</a>-18;
