<a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/120/#laws.3.3.0">2024 c 120 art 3 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/121/#laws.4.7.0">2024 c 121 art 4 s 7</a></p>
      </div>
      <p><b>NOTE: </b>Subdivision 8 was also amended by Laws 2024, chapter 120, article 3, section 3, to read as follows:</p>
      <div class="subd" id="stat.326.10.8">
        <a title='Link to "Subd. 8.' class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.10.8">§</a>
        <h2 class="subd_no">"Subd. 8.<span class="headnote">Expiration and renewal.</span></h2>
        <p>(a) All licenses and certificates, other than in-training certificates, issued by the board expire at midnight on June 30 of each even-numbered calendar year if not renewed. A holder of a license or certificate issued by the board may renew it by completing and filing with the board an application for renewal consisting of a fully completed form provided by the board and the fee specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.105">326.105</a>. Both the fee and the application must be submitted at the same time and by June 30 of each even-numbered calendar year. The form must be signed by the applicant, contain all of the information requested, and clearly show that the licensee or certificate holder has completed the minimum number of required professional development hours or has been granted an exemption under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.107#stat.326.107.4">326.107, subdivision 4</a>. An application for renewal that does not comply with the requirements of this subdivision is an incomplete application and must not be accepted by the board.</p>
        <p>(b) No later than 30 days before the expiration of a license or certificate, the board must send the holder of the license or certificate a notice by email that the license or certificate is about to expire. The notice must include information on the process and requirements for renewal. The application form for a new or renewed license or certificate issued by the board must request that the applicant provide an email address for the purpose of providing this notice. If the board does not have a record of a license or certificate holder's email address, the board must send the notice to the holder by standard mail."</p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.326.105">
        <h1 class="shn">326.105 FEES.</h1>
        <p>The fee for licensure or renewal of licensure as an architect, professional engineer, land surveyor, landscape architect, or geoscience professional is $120 per biennium. The fee for certification as a certified interior designer or for renewal of the certificate is $120 per biennium. The fee for an architect applying for original certification as a certified interior designer is $50 per biennium. The initial license or certification fee for all professions is $120. The renewal fee shall be paid biennially on or before June 30 of each even-numbered year. The renewal fee, when paid by mail, is not timely paid unless it is postmarked on or before June 30 of each even-numbered year. The application fee is $25 for in-training applicants and $75 for professional license applicants.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.326.105.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1999/0/213/#laws.0.1.0">1999 c 213 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1999/0/223/#laws.2.59.0">1999 c 223 art 2 s 59</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2000/0/488/#laws.2.20.0">2000 c 488 art 2 s 20</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2006/0/282/#laws.11.12.0">2006 c 282 art 11 s 12</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.326.106">
        <h1 class="shn">326.106 DEFINITIONS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.106.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.106.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Scope.</span></h2>
          <p>The terms used in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.107">326.107</a> have the meanings given them in this section.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.106.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.106.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Biennial renewal.</span></h2>
          <p>"Biennial renewal" means a period of time consisting of two consecutive 12-month periods ending on June 30 of each even-numbered year.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.106.3">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.106.3">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Dual license or certificate holder.</span></h2>
          <p>"Dual license or certificate holder" means a person who is licensed or certified in two professions regulated by the board.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.106.4">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.106.4">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Professional development hours.</span></h2>
          <p>"Professional development hours" means contact hours consisting of not less than 50 minutes each of instruction or presentation meeting the requirements of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.107">326.107</a>.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.326.106.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1999/0/213/#laws.0.2.0">1999 c 213 s 2</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.326.107">
        <h1 class="shn">326.107 CONTINUING EDUCATION.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.107.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.107.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Requirements.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Architects, professional engineers, land surveyors, landscape architects, geoscientists, and interior designers licensed or certified by this board must obtain the number of professional development hours described in paragraphs (b) to (d) during each two-year period of licensure or certification identified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.105">326.105</a>. This requirement must be satisfied during the two-year period prior to biennial renewal except for a carryover permitted from the previous renewal period, which must not exceed 50 percent of the biennial requirement of professional development hours.</p>
          <p>(b) Licensed professional engineers must earn a minimum of 24 professional development hours, of which two must be dedicated to professional ethics, per biennial renewal except for the carryover permitted. The ethics hours must have been earned during the biennium to which they are applied and shall not be used toward carryover.</p>
          <p>(c) Licensed architects, land surveyors, landscape architects, geoscientists, and certified interior designers must earn a minimum of 24 professional development hours, of which two must be dedicated to professional ethics, per biennial renewal except for the carryover permitted. The ethics hours must have been earned during the biennium to which they are applied and shall not be used toward carryover.</p>
          <p>(d) Dual license or certificate holders who have obtained a license or certificate for two professions must earn professional development hours required by the license or certificate requiring the greatest number of professional development hours and must obtain in each profession a minimum of one-third of the total professional development hours required. The remaining one-third requirement may be obtained in either profession at the sole discretion of the licensee or certificate holder.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.107.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.107.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Programs and activities.</span></h2>
          <p>Continuing education must consist of learning experiences which enhance and expand the skills, knowledge, and abilities of practicing professionals to remain current and render competent professional services to the public. Practitioners may pursue technical, nontechnical, regulatory, ethical, and business practice needs for a well-rounded education provided the education directly benefits the health, safety, or welfare of the public. Continuing education activities which satisfy the professional development requirement include, but are not limited to, the following:</p>
          <p>(1) completing or auditing college-sponsored courses;</p>
          <p>(2) completing self-study college or non-college-sponsored courses, presented by correspondence, Internet, television, video, or audio, ending with examination or other verification processes;</p>
          <p>(3) participation in seminars, tutorials, televised or videotaped courses, or short courses;</p>
          <p>(4) attending self-sponsored and prepared in-house educational programs;</p>
          <p>(5) completing a study tour with a structured program resulting in a written or visual presentation by the licensee or certificate holder;</p>
          <p>(6) presenting or instructing qualifying courses or seminars. Professional development hours may be earned for preparation time for the initial presentation;</p>
          <p>(7) authoring published papers, articles, or books. Professional development hours earned may equal preparation time spent, may be claimed only following publication, and shall be given for authorship or presentation, but not for both;</p>
          <p>(8) participating in professional examination grading or writing. A maximum of five professional development hours per biennium may be applied from this source. The hours from this source must have been earned during the biennium to which they are applied and shall not be used toward carryover;</p>
          <p>(9) providing professional service to the public which draws upon the licensee's or certificate holder's professional expertise on boards, commissions, and committees such as planning commissions, building code advisory boards, urban renewal boards, or non-work-related volunteer service. A maximum of ten professional development hours per biennium may be applied from this source regardless of the number of boards, commissions, and committees the licensee or certificate holder serves. The hours from this source must have been earned during the biennium to which they are applied and shall not be used toward carryover; and</p>
          <p>(10) patents, after they are granted, for a credit of ten professional development hours. The patent must have been granted during the biennium to which the hours are applied and shall not be used toward carryover.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.107.3">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.107.3">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Criteria.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Continuing education courses and activities must meet the criteria in paragraphs (b) to (f).</p>
          <p>(b) There must be a clear purpose and objective for each activity which will maintain, improve, or expand skills and knowledge obtained prior to initial licensure or certification or develop new and relevant skills and knowledge.</p>
          <p>(c) The content of each presentation must be well organized and presented in a sequential manner.</p>
          <p>(d) There must be evidence of preplanning which must include the opportunity for input by the target group to be served.</p>
          <p>(e) The presentation must be made by persons who are well qualified by education or experience.</p>
          <p>(f) There must be a provision for documentation of the individual's participation in the activity, including information required for record keeping and reporting.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.107.4">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.107.4">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Exemptions.</span></h2>
          <p>The following licensees or certificate holders are exempt from the continuing education requirements:</p>
          <p>(1) a new licensee or certificate holder for the individual's first biennial renewal; or</p>
          <p>(2) a licensee or certificate holder who has experienced during the biennial renewal a serious illness, injury, or other extenuating circumstances, or who has been called to active duty in the military services for a period of time exceeding 120 consecutive days, as reviewed and approved by the board, and where such activities restrict compliance with the continuing education requirements, as supported by documentation furnished to the board.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.107.5">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 5." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.107.5">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/236/#laws.0.13.0">2014 c 236 s 13</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.107.6">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 6." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.107.6">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/0/85/#laws.0.5.0">2003 c 85 s 5</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.107.7">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 7." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.107.7">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Reports and records.</span></h2>
          <p>The licensee or certificate holder shall maintain a file in which records of courses and activities are kept, including dates, subjects, duration of programs, sponsoring organization, professional development hours earned, registration receipts where appropriate, and other pertinent documentation, for a period of four years after the end of the licensure period for which the hours apply. This information may be required to be produced by licensees or certificate holders. The board may require a licensee or certificate holder to produce this information in connection with verification of a renewal application, a random audit conducted by the board, or upon receipt of a complaint alleging noncompliance on the part of a licensee or certificate holder.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.107.8">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 8." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.107.8">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.<span class="headnote">Noncompliance.</span></h2>
          <p>If the board rejects professional development hours reported by a licensee or certificate holder in an amount sufficient to reduce the number of nonrejected professional development hours below the required minimum number, the licensee or certificate holder must be notified of the board's rejection of the hours. The licensee or certificate holder has 180 days after notification to substantiate the validity of the rejected hours or to earn other qualifying hours to meet the minimum requirement. The board's rejection of any professional development hours submitted during this 180-day cure period does not extend or expand the cure period. If the board does not reinstate a sufficient number of the rejected professional development hours to meet the required minimum number of professional development hours, or the licensee or certificate holder does not complete or substantiate that the individual has completed other qualifying professional development hours to meet the required minimum number of professional development hours within the specified period of time, the individual's licensure or certification shall be suspended. Professional development hours earned within the 180-day cure period and applied to current renewal may not be applied to the requirements for the following biennial renewal.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.107.9">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 9." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.107.9">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 9.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/0/85/#laws.0.5.0">2003 c 85 s 5</a>]</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.326.107.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1999/0/213/#laws.0.3.0">1999 c 213 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/0/85/#laws.0.3.0">2003 c 85 s 3</a>,4; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/236/#laws.0.8.0">2014 c 236 s 8</a>-10</p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.326.11">
        <h1 class="shn">326.11 LICENSE AND CERTIFICATE REGULATION.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.11.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.11.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Revocation or suspension.</span></h2>
          <p>The board shall have the power to revoke or suspend the license or certificate of any architect, engineer, land surveyor, landscape architect, geoscientist, or certified interior designer, who is found guilty by the board of any fraud or deceit in obtaining a license or certificate, or of attaching the licensee's or certificate holder's seal or signature to any plan, specification, report, plat, or other architectural, engineering, land surveying, landscape architectural, geoscientific, or interior design document not prepared by the person signing or sealing it or under that person's direct supervision, or of gross negligence, incompetency, or misconduct in the practice of architecture, engineering, land surveying, landscape architecture, geoscience, or interior design, or upon conviction of any violation of sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.02">326.02</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.15">326.15</a> or amendments thereof, or of any crime involving moral turpitude or upon adjudication of insanity or incompetency.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.11.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.11.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1976/0/222/#laws.0.209.0">1976 c 222 s 209</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.11.3">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.11.3">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1976/0/222/#laws.0.209.0">1976 c 222 s 209</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.11.4">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.11.4">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1976/0/222/#laws.0.209.0">1976 c 222 s 209</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.11.5">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 5." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.11.5">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Reissuance.</span></h2>
          <p>The board may reissue a license to any person whose license has been suspended or revoked upon application for relicensure.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.11.6">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 6." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.11.6">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Replacement.</span></h2>
          <p>A new license to replace any license revoked, lost, destroyed, or mutilated, may be issued, subject to the rules of the board.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.326.11.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">(<a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1940A/cite/35">5697-10</a>) <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1921/0/523/#laws.0.10.0">1921 c 523 s 10</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1945/0/380/#laws.0.4.0">1945 c 380 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1949/0/86/#laws.0.4.0">1949 c 86 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1967/1/28/#laws.0.8.0">Ex1967 c 28 s 8</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1975/0/329/#laws.0.13.0">1975 c 329 s 13</a>-16; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1976/0/222/#laws.0.146.0">1976 c 222 s 146</a>-148; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1978/0/514/#laws.0.1.0">1978 c 514 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1981/1/4/#laws.1.32.0">1Sp1981 c 4 art 1 s 32</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/0/444/#laws.0.0.0">1986 c 444</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1992/0/507/#laws.0.17.0">1992 c 507 s 17</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/0/206/#laws.0.19.0">1995 c 206 s 19</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.326.111">
        <h1 class="shn">326.111 UNAUTHORIZED PRACTICE; DISCIPLINARY ACTION.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.111.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.111.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Generally.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) If the board, or the complaint committee if authorized by the board, has a reasonable basis to believe that a person has engaged in an act or practice constituting the unauthorized practice of architecture, engineering, land surveying, landscape architecture, geoscience, or the unauthorized use of the title certified interior designer, or a violation of a statute, rule, or order that the board has issued or is empowered to enforce, the board, or the complaint committee if authorized by the board, may proceed as described in subdivisions 2 and 3.</p>
          <p>(b) The board shall establish a complaint committee to investigate, mediate, or initiate administrative or legal proceedings on behalf of the board with respect to complaints filed with or information received by the board alleging or indicating the unauthorized practice of architecture, engineering, land surveying, landscape architecture, geoscience, or the unauthorized use of the title certified interior designer, or a violation of statute, rule, or order that the board has issued or is empowered to enforce. The complaint committee shall consist of five members of the board, with no more than one from each of the professions licensed by the board, and no more than two public members.</p>
          <p>(c) Except as otherwise described in this section, all hearings shall be conducted in accordance with chapter 14.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.111.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.111.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Legal action.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) When necessary to prevent the unauthorized practice of architecture, engineering, land surveying, landscape architecture, geoscience, or the unauthorized use of the title certified interior designer, or a violation of a statute, rule, or order that the board has issued or is empowered to enforce, the board, or the complaint committee if authorized by the board, may bring an action in the name of the state in the district court in Ramsey County or in any county in which jurisdiction is proper to enjoin the act, practice, or violation and to enforce compliance with the statute, rule, or order. Upon a showing that a person has engaged in an act or practice constituting the unauthorized practice of architecture, engineering, land surveying, landscape architecture, geoscience, or the unauthorized use of the title certified interior designer, or a violation of a statute, rule, or order that the board has issued or is empowered to enforce, a permanent or temporary injunction, restraining order, or other appropriate relief shall be granted.</p>
          <p>(b) For purposes of injunctive relief under this subdivision, irreparable harm exists when the board shows that a person has engaged in an act or practice constituting the unauthorized practice of architecture, engineering, land surveying, landscape architecture, geoscience, or the unauthorized use of the title certified interior designer, or a violation of a statute, rule, or order that the board has issued or is empowered to enforce.</p>
          <p>(c) Injunctive relief granted under paragraph (a) does not relieve an enjoined person from criminal prosecution by a competent authority or from disciplinary action by the board with respect to the person's license, certificate, or application for examination, license, or renewal.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.111.3">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.111.3">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Cease and desist orders.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The board, or the complaint committee if authorized by the board, may issue and have served upon a person an order requiring the person to cease and desist from the unauthorized practice of architecture, engineering, land surveying, landscape architecture, geoscience, or the unauthorized use of the titles architect, professional engineer, land surveyor, landscape architect, professional geologist, professional soil scientist, certified interior designer, or violation of the statute, rule, or order. The order shall be calculated to give reasonable notice of the rights of the person to request a hearing and shall state the reasons for the entry of the order.</p>
          <p>(b) Service of the order is effective if the order is served on the person or counsel of record personally or by certified mail to the most recent address provided to the board for the person or counsel of record.</p>
          <p>(c) Unless otherwise agreed by the board, or the complaint committee if authorized by the board, and the person requesting the hearing, the hearing shall be held no later than 30 days after the request for the hearing is received by the board.</p>
          <p>(d) The administrative law judge shall issue a report within 30 days of the close of the contested case hearing record, notwithstanding Minnesota Rules, part <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/rules/1400.8100">1400.8100</a>, subpart 3. Within 30 days after receiving the report and any exceptions to it, the board shall issue a further order vacating, modifying, or making permanent the cease and desist orders as the facts require.</p>
          <p>(e) If no hearing is requested within 30 days of service of the order, the order becomes final and remains in effect until it is modified or vacated by the board.</p>
          <p>(f) If the person to whom a cease and desist order is issued fails to appear at the hearing after being duly notified, the person is in default and the proceeding may be determined against that person upon consideration of the cease and desist order, the allegations of which may be considered to be true.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.111.4">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.111.4">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Actions against applicants and licensees.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The board may, by order, deny, refuse to renew, suspend, temporarily suspend, or revoke the application, license, or certification of a person; censure or reprimand that person; condition or limit the person's practice; refuse to permit a person to sit for examination; or refuse to release the person's examination grades if the board finds that the order is in the public interest and the applicant, licensee, or certificate holder:</p>
          <p>(1) has violated a statute, rule, or order that the board has issued or is empowered to enforce;</p>
          <p>(2) has engaged in conduct or acts that are fraudulent, deceptive, or dishonest whether or not the conduct or acts relate to the practice of architecture, engineering, land surveying, landscape architecture, geoscience, or certified interior design, providing that the fraudulent, deceptive, or dishonest conduct or acts reflect adversely on the person's ability or fitness to engage in the practice of architecture, engineering, land surveying, landscape architecture, geoscience, or certified interior design;</p>
          <p>(3) has engaged in conduct or acts that are negligent or otherwise in violation of the standards established by Minnesota Rules, chapters 1800 and 1805, where the conduct or acts relate to the practice of architecture, engineering, land surveying, landscape architecture, geoscience, or use of the title certified interior designer;</p>
          <p>(4) has been convicted of or has pled guilty or nolo contendere to a felony, an element of which is dishonesty or fraud, whether or not the person admits guilt, or has been shown to have engaged in acts or practices tending to show that the applicant or licensee is incompetent or has engaged in conduct reflecting adversely on the person's ability or fitness to engage in the practice of architecture, engineering, land surveying, landscape architecture, geoscience, or use of the title certified interior designer;</p>
          <p>(5) employed fraud or deception in obtaining a certificate, license, renewal, or reinstatement or in passing all or a portion of the examination;</p>
          <p>(6) has had the person's architecture, engineering, land surveying, landscape architecture, geoscience, or interior design license, certificate, right to examine, or other similar authority revoked, suspended, canceled, limited, or not renewed for cause in any state, commonwealth, or territory of the United States, in the District of Columbia, or in any foreign country;</p>
          <p>(7) has had the person's right to practice before any federal, state, or other government agency revoked, suspended, canceled, limited, or not renewed;</p>
          <p>(8) failed to meet any requirement for the issuance or renewal of the person's license or certificate;</p>
          <p>(9) has attached the person's seal or signature to a plan, specification, report, plat, or other architectural, engineering, land surveying, landscape architectural, geoscientific, or interior design document not prepared by the person sealing or signing it or under that person's direct supervision; or</p>
          <p>(10) with respect to temporary suspension orders, has committed an act, engaged in conduct, or committed practices that may, or has in the opinion of the board, or the complaint committee if authorized by the board, resulted in an immediate threat to the public.</p>
          <p>(b) In lieu of or in addition to any remedy provided in paragraph (a), the board may require, as a condition of continued licensure, possession of certificate, termination of suspension, reinstatement of license or certificate, examination, or release of examination grades, that the person:</p>
          <p>(1) submit to a quality review of the person's ability, skills, or quality of work, conducted in such fashion and by such persons, entity, or entities as the board may require including, but not limited to, remedial education courses; and</p>
          <p>(2) complete to the satisfaction of the board such continuing professional education courses as the board may specify by rule.</p>
          <p>(c) Service of the order is effective if the order is served on the licensee, certificate holder, applicant, person, or counsel of record personally or by certified mail, to the most recent address provided to the board for the licensee, certificate holder, applicant, person, or counsel of record. The order shall state the reasons for the entry of the order.</p>
          <p>(d) All hearings required by this section shall be conducted in accordance with chapter 14, except with respect to temporary suspension orders, as provided for in subdivision 5, paragraph (d).</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.111.5">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 5." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.111.5">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Procedure for temporary suspension of license or certificate.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) When the board, or the complaint committee if authorized by the board, issues a temporary suspension order, the suspension is in effect upon service of a written order on the licensee or counsel of record, specifying the statute, rule, or order violated. The order remains in effect until the board issues a final order in the matter after a hearing or upon agreement between the board and the licensee.</p>
          <p>(b) Service of the order is effective if the order is served on the licensee or counsel of record personally or by certified mail, to the most recent address provided to the board for the licensee or counsel of record.</p>
          <p>(c) The order shall set forth the rights to a hearing contained in this subdivision and shall state the reasons for the entry of the order.</p>
          <p>(d) Within ten days after service of the order, the licensee may request a hearing in writing. The board shall hold a hearing before its own members within five working days of receipt of a request for hearing on the sole issue of whether there is a reasonable basis to continue, modify, or lift the temporary suspension. This hearing is not subject to chapter 14. Evidence presented by the board or the licensee shall be in affidavit form only. The licensee or counsel of record may appear for oral argument.</p>
          <p>(e) Within five working days after the hearing, the board shall issue its order and, if the suspension is continued, schedule a contested case hearing within 30 days after issuance of the order. The administrative law judge shall issue a report within 30 days after closing of the contested case hearing record, notwithstanding the provisions of Minnesota Rules, part <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/rules/1400.8100">1400.8100</a>, subpart 3. The board shall issue a final order within 30 days after receipt of that report and any exceptions to it.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.111.6">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 6." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.111.6">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Violations; penalties; costs of proceeding.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The board may impose a civil penalty not to exceed $10,000 per violation upon a person who commits an act or practice constituting the unauthorized practice of architecture, engineering, land surveying, landscape architecture, geoscience, or the unauthorized use of the title certified interior designer, or violates a statute, rule, or order that the board has issued or is empowered to enforce.</p>
          <p>(b) The board may, in addition, impose a fee to reimburse the board for all or part of the cost of the proceedings resulting in disciplinary action authorized by this section, the imposition of civil penalties, or the issuance of a cease and desist order. The fee may be imposed when the board shows that the position of the person who commits an act or practice constituting the unauthorized practice of architecture, engineering, land surveying, landscape architecture, geoscience, or the unauthorized use of the title certified interior designer, or violates a statute, rule, or order that the board has issued or is empowered to enforce is not substantially justified, unless special circumstances make an award unjust, notwithstanding the provisions of Minnesota Rules, part <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/rules/1400.8401">1400.8401</a>. The costs include, but are not limited to, the amount paid by the board for services from the office of administrative hearings, attorney fees, court reporters, witnesses, reproduction of records, board members' per diem compensation, board staff time, and expense incurred by board members and staff.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.111.7">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 7." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.111.7">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Reinstatement.</span></h2>
          <p>The board may reinstate a suspended, revoked, or surrendered certificate or license upon petition of the former or suspended certificate holder or licensee. The board may, in its sole discretion, place any other conditions, including reexamination in whole or in part, upon reinstatement of a suspended, revoked, or surrendered certificate or license that it finds appropriate and necessary to ensure that the purposes of sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.02">326.02</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.15">326.15</a> are met. No suspended certificate or license shall be reinstated until the former certificate holder or licensee has completed one-half of the suspension.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.326.111.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/358/#laws.0.1.0">1993 c 358 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1994/0/465/#laws.1.39.0">1994 c 465 art 1 s 39</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/0/206/#laws.0.20.0">1995 c 206 s 20</a>-24; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1999/0/213/#laws.0.4.0">1999 c 213 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/236/#laws.0.11.0">2014 c 236 s 11</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.326.12">
        <h1 class="shn">326.12 LICENSE OR CERTIFICATE AS EVIDENCE; SEAL.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.12.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.12.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Judicial proof.</span></h2>
          <p>The issuance of a license or certificate by the board shall be evidence that the person named therein is entitled to all the rights and privileges of a licensed architect, licensed engineer, licensed land surveyor, licensed landscape architect, licensed geoscientist, or certified interior designer while the license or certificate remains unrevoked or has not expired or has not been suspended.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.12.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.12.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Seal.</span></h2>
          <p>Each licensee or certificate holder may, upon licensure or certification, obtain a seal bearing the licensee's or certificate holder's name and the legend "licensed architect," "licensed professional engineer," "licensed land surveyor," "licensed landscape architect," the appropriate licensed professional geoscientist legend as defined by the board, or "certified interior designer." Plans, specifications, plats, reports, and other documents prepared by a licensee or certificate holder may be stamped with the seal during the life of the license or certificate. A rubber stamp facsimile thereof may be used in lieu of the seal on tracings from which prints are to be made or on papers which would be damaged by the regular seal. It shall be unlawful for any one to stamp or seal any document with the stamp or seal after the license or certificate has expired, been revoked or suspended, unless said license or certificate shall have been renewed or reissued.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.12.3">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.12.3">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Certified signature.</span></h2>
          <p>Each plan, drawing, specification, plat, report, or other document which under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.02">326.02</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.15">326.15</a> is prepared by a licensed architect, licensed engineer, licensed land surveyor, licensed landscape architect, licensed geoscientist, or certified interior designer must bear the signature of the licensed or certified person preparing it, or the signature of the licensed or certified person under whose direct supervision it was prepared. Each signature shall be accompanied by a certification that the signer is licensed or certified under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.02">326.02</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.15">326.15</a>, by the person's license or certificate number, and by the date on which the signature was affixed. The provisions of this paragraph shall not apply to any plans, drawings, specifications, plats, reports, or other documents of an intraoffice or intracompany nature or that are considered to be drafts or of a preliminary, schematic, or design development nature by licensed or certified individuals who would normally be responsible for their preparation. The required signature and certification must appear on all pages of plans and drawings that must be signed, but only on the first page of specifications, plats, reports, or other documents that must be signed. A stamp, printed signature, or electronically created signature has the same force and effect as an actual signature if it creates an accurate depiction of the licensed or certified professional's actual signature.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.326.12.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">(<a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1940A/cite/35">5697-11</a>) <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1921/0/523/#laws.0.11.0">1921 c 523 s 11</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1945/0/380/#laws.0.5.0">1945 c 380 s 5</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1971/0/22/#laws.0.6.0">1971 c 22 s 6</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1975/0/329/#laws.0.17.0">1975 c 329 s 17</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1976/0/222/#laws.0.149.0">1976 c 222 s 149</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1981/1/4/#laws.1.33.0">1Sp1981 c 4 art 1 s 33</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1992/0/507/#laws.0.18.0">1992 c 507 s 18</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1994/0/632/#laws.3.55.0">1994 c 632 art 3 s 55</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/0/206/#laws.0.25.0">1995 c 206 s 25</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/0/265/#laws.2.28.0">1995 c 265 art 2 s 28</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2002/0/245/#laws.0.1.0">2002 c 245 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2004/0/228/#laws.1.60.0">2004 c 228 art 1 s 60</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/236/#laws.0.12.0">2014 c 236 s 12</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.326.13">
        <h1 class="shn">326.13 PRACTICE EXEMPT.</h1>
        <p>Practice of architecture, engineering, landscape architecture, land surveying, or geoscience, or use of the title certified interior designer in this state prior to licensure or certification by the board shall be permitted under the following conditions and limitations:</p>
        <p>(1) By any person or firm not a resident of and having no established place of business in this state, or any person or firm resident in this state, but whose arrival in the state is recent; provided, however, such person or a person connected with such firm:</p>
        <p>(i) is registered or licensed and qualified to practice such profession in a state or country to which the board grants licensure or certification by comity in accordance with the provisions of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.10#stat.326.10.1">326.10, subdivision 1</a>, clause (2); and</p>
        <p>(ii) shall have filed an application for licensure as an architect, an engineer, a geoscientist, or a certified interior designer shall have paid the fee provided for in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.10">326.10</a>, and shall have been notified by the board that the applicant meets the requirements for licensure or certification in this state and is entitled to receive a license or certificate, and has applied for and been granted a temporary permit to practice. Temporary permits shall be granted to do a specific job for the period stipulated on the permit.</p>
        <p>(2) By a nonresident applicant who seeks to provide architecture, engineering, land surveying, landscape architecture, geoscience, or certified interior design services in this state if the applicant offers to practice only for the purpose of seeking to provide services, without having first been registered or certified by the state, if the applicant:</p>
        <p>(i) is registered and qualified to practice such profession in a state or country to which the board grants registration or licensure by comity in accordance with section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.10#stat.326.10.1">326.10, subdivision 1</a>, clause (2);</p>
        <p>(ii) notified the board in writing that the applicant is not currently registered in this state, but will be present in this state for the purpose of seeking to provide services;</p>
        <p>(iii) delivers a copy of the notice referred to in item (ii) to every potential client for whom the applicant is seeking to provide services; and</p>
        <p>(iv) applies within ten days to the board for licensure or certification if selected as the design professional for a project in this state; the applicant is prohibited from actually rendering services as defined within the terms of sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.02">326.02</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.15">326.15</a> until the applicant is licensed or certified, or obtains a temporary permit as described in clause (1).</p>
        <p>(3) Practice as an architect, an engineer, a land surveyor, a landscape architect, or a geoscientist, or use of the title certified interior designer solely as an officer or employee of the United States.</p>
        <p>(4) Practice as a geoscientist by a person who would be qualified under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.02">326.02</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.15">326.15</a> by virtue of experience and education while (i) engaged in exploration, development, extraction, and reclamation of minerals and mineral deposits or energy resources including sand, gravel, peat, industrial minerals, metallic minerals, iron ore, coal, oil, and gas and other mineral fuels; (ii) an employee of a corporation or agency engaged in such exploration, development, extraction, and reclamation of minerals and mineral deposits; (iii) acting in accordance with the provisions of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/82B.035#stat.82B.035.3">82B.035, subdivision 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/103I.205#stat.103I.205.4">103I.205, subdivision 4</a>; or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/103I.601#stat.103I.601.2">103I.601, subdivision 2</a>; or (iv) engaged in academic geoscience research.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.326.13.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">(<a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1940A/cite/35">5697-13</a>) <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1921/0/523/#laws.0.13.0">1921 c 523 s 13</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1933/0/404/#laws.0.4.0">1933 c 404 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1971/0/22/#laws.0.7.0">1971 c 22 s 7</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1975/0/329/#laws.0.18.0">1975 c 329 s 18</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1976/0/222/#laws.0.150.0">1976 c 222 s 150</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1981/1/4/#laws.1.34.0">1Sp1981 c 4 art 1 s 34</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/0/444/#laws.0.0.0">1986 c 444</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1992/0/507/#laws.0.19.0">1992 c 507 s 19</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/0/206/#laws.0.26.0">1995 c 206 s 26</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1998/0/324/#laws.0.7.0">1998 c 324 s 7</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2004/0/228/#laws.1.61.0">2004 c 228 art 1 s 61</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.326.14">
        <h1 class="shn">326.14 CORPORATIONS AND PARTNERSHIPS AUTHORIZED.</h1>
        <p>A corporation, partnership or other firm may engage in work of an architectural or engineering character, in land surveying, in landscape architecture, or in geoscience, or use the title of certified interior designer in this state, provided the person or persons connected with such corporation, partnership or other firm in responsible charge of such work is or are licensed or certified as herein required for the practice of architecture, engineering, land surveying, landscape architecture, and geoscience, and use of the title of certified interior designer.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.326.14.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">(<a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1940A/cite/35">5697-14</a>) <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1921/0/523/#laws.0.14.0">1921 c 523 s 14</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1933/0/404/#laws.0.5.0">1933 c 404 s 5</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1945/0/380/#laws.0.6.0">1945 c 380 s 6</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1975/0/329/#laws.0.19.0">1975 c 329 s 19</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1976/0/222/#laws.0.151.0">1976 c 222 s 151</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1992/0/507/#laws.0.20.0">1992 c 507 s 20</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/0/206/#laws.0.27.0">1995 c 206 s 27</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.326.15">
        <h1 class="shn">326.15 FALSE IMPERSONATION.</h1>
        <p>It shall be unlawful for any person to present or attempt to use as the person's own the seal or certificate of another, or to give false or forged evidence of any kind to the board, or any member thereof, or to falsely impersonate any licensee or certificate holder of like or different name, or to use or attempt to use as the person's own the license of another issued by any authority outside of this state, or to use or attempt to use an expired or revoked or suspended license.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.326.15.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">(<a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1940A/cite/35">5697-15</a>) <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1921/0/523/#laws.0.15.0">1921 c 523 s 15</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1945/0/380/#laws.0.7.0">1945 c 380 s 7</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1976/0/222/#laws.0.152.0">1976 c 222 s 152</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/0/444/#laws.0.0.0">1986 c 444</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2004/0/228/#laws.1.62.0">2004 c 228 art 1 s 62</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.326.16"><b>326.16</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1976/0/222/#laws.0.209.0">1976 c 222 s 209</a>]</div>

      <h1 class="fgh">PRIVATE DETECTIVES, PROTECTIVE AGENTS</h1>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.326.32"><b>326.32</b> MS 1965 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1967/0/602/#laws.0.17.0">1967 c 602 s 17</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.326.32">
        <h1 class="shn">326.32 DEFINITIONS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.32.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.32.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Scope.</span></h2>
          <p>As used in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.32">326.32</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.339">326.339</a>, the terms defined in this section have the meanings given them.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.32.1a">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 1a." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.32.1a">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 1a.<span class="headnote">Armed with a firearm.</span></h2>
          <p>An individual is "armed with a firearm" if at any time in the performance of the individual's duties the individual wears, carries, possesses, or has access to a firearm.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.32.1b">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 1b." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.32.1b">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 1b.<span class="headnote">Armed with a weapon.</span></h2>
          <p>An individual is "armed with a weapon" if at any time in the performance of the individual's duties the individual wears, carries, possesses, or has access to:</p>
          <p>(1) a weapon other than a firearm; or</p>
          <p>(2) an immobilizing or restraining device.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.32.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.32.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Board.</span></h2>
          <p>"Board" means the Board of Private Detective and Protective Agent Services.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.32.3">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.32.3">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Board member.</span></h2>
          <p>"Board member" means any person appointed as a member of the Board of Private Detective and Protective Agent Services.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.32.4">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.32.4">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Chair.</span></h2>
          <p>"Chair" means the board member designated by the board to act in the capacity of board chair.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.32.5">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 5." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.32.5">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Board review.</span></h2>
          <p>"Board review" means the process by which the board reviews and evaluates private detective or protective agent license applications.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.32.6">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 6." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.32.6">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/360/#laws.0.26.0">1987 c 360 s 26</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.32.7">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 7." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.32.7">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/360/#laws.0.26.0">1987 c 360 s 26</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.32.8">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 8." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.32.8">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.<span class="headnote">Applicant.</span></h2>
          <p>"Applicant" means any individual, partnership or corporation who has made application for a private detective or protective agent license.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.32.9">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 9." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.32.9">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 9.<span class="headnote">License.</span></h2>
          <p>"License" means a private detective license or a protective agent license.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.32.10">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 10." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.32.10">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 10.<span class="headnote">License holder.</span></h2>
          <p>"License holder" means any individual, partnership as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/323A.0101">323A.0101</a>, clause (8), or corporation licensed to perform the duties of a private detective or a protective agent.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.32.10a">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 10a." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.32.10a">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 10a.<span class="headnote">Minnesota manager.</span></h2>
          <p>"Minnesota manager" means the member of a partnership or corporation, who meets the qualifications for licensing as provided in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.32">326.32</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.339">326.339</a>. The Minnesota manager must be actively involved in the day to day management and supervision of the licensed activity in the Minnesota office.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.32.10b">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 10b." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.32.10b">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 10b.<span class="headnote">Minnesota office.</span></h2>
          <p>"Minnesota office" means an office maintained in Minnesota by a license holder for the conduct or solicitation of business when the principal place of business of the license holder is located outside the state of Minnesota.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.32.10c">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 10c." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.32.10c">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 10c.<span class="headnote">Proprietary employer.</span></h2>
          <p>A "proprietary employer" means an individual, partnership, or corporation that is not engaged in the business of providing protective agents but employs individuals to serve as security guards solely on the employer's property and its curtilage.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.32.11">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 11." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.32.11">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 11.<span class="headnote">Public member.</span></h2>
          <p>"Public member" means a person who is not, nor ever was, a member of the profession or occupation being licensed, or the spouse of any such person or a person who has not, nor ever has had, a material or financial interest in either the providing of the professional service being licensed or regulated or an activity directly related to the profession being licensed.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.32.12">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 12." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.32.12">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 12.<span class="headnote">Qualified representative.</span></h2>
          <p>"Qualified representative" means the member of a partnership or corporation, who meets the qualifications for licensing as provided in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.32">326.32</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.339">326.339</a>. The qualified representative must be actively involved in the day to day management and supervision of the licensed activity.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.32.13">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 13." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.32.13">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 13.<span class="headnote">Security guard.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) "Security guard" means a person who wears or carries any insignia that identifies the person to the public as security, who is paid a fee, wage, or salary to do one or more of the following:</p>
          <p>(1) prevent or detect intrusion, unauthorized entry or activity, vandalism, or trespass on private property;</p>
          <p>(2) prevent or detect theft, loss, embezzlement, misappropriation, or concealment of merchandise, money, bonds, stocks, notes, or other valuable documents or papers;</p>
          <p>(3) control, regulate, or direct the flow or movements of the public, whether by vehicle or otherwise, to assure protection of private property;</p>
          <p>(4) protect individuals from bodily harm; or</p>
          <p>(5) enforce policies and rules of the security guard's employer related to crime reduction to the extent that the enforcement falls within the scope of the security guard's duties.</p>
          <p>(b) The term "security guard" does not include:</p>
          <p>(1) an auditor, accountant, or accounting clerk performing audits or accounting functions;</p>
          <p>(2) an employee of a firm licensed under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.3381">326.3381</a> whose duties are primarily administrative or clerical in nature;</p>
          <p>(3) a person employed by a proprietary company to conduct plainclothes surveillance or investigation;</p>
          <p>(4) a person temporarily employed under statute or ordinance by political subdivisions to provide protective services at social functions;</p>
          <p>(5) an employee of an air or rail carrier;</p>
          <p>(6) a customer service representative or sales clerk employed in a retail establishment; or</p>
          <p>(7) a person employed to perform primarily maintenance or custodial functions.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.32.14">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 14." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.32.14">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 14.</h2>
          <p>[Renumbered subd 1a]</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.326.32.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1974/0/310/#laws.0.1.0">1974 c 310 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1975/0/271/#laws.0.6.0">1975 c 271 s 6</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1984/0/649/#laws.0.5.0">1984 c 649 s 5</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/0/444/#laws.0.0.0">1986 c 444</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/360/#laws.0.1.0">1987 c 360 s 1</a>-6; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/171/#laws.0.1.0">1989 c 171 s 1</a>,2; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1990/0/485/#laws.0.1.0">1990 c 485 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2001/0/168/#laws.0.1.0">2001 c 168 s 1</a>,2; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/52/#laws.5.44.0">2023 c 52 art 5 s 44</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.326.33"><b>326.33</b> MS 1965 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1945/0/130/#laws.0.1.0">1945 c 130 s 1</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.326.33">
        <h1 class="shn">326.33 BOARD OF PRIVATE DETECTIVE AND PROTECTIVE AGENT SERVICES.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.33.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.33.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Members.</span></h2>
          <p>There is hereby created a Board of Private Detective and Protective Agent Services, consisting of the superintendent of the Bureau of Criminal Apprehension or an assistant superintendent designated by the superintendent, and the following members appointed by the commissioner of public safety: a licensed protective agent, or qualified representative for a licensed protective agent partnership or corporation, a licensed private detective, or qualified representative for a licensed private detective partnership or corporation, and two public members. Filling of member vacancies shall be the responsibility of the commissioner of public safety. Membership terms, compensation of members, removal of members, the filling of membership vacancies, and fiscal year and reporting requirements shall be as provided in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/214.07">214.07</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/214.09">214.09</a>. The provision of staff, unless otherwise provided in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.32">326.32</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.339">326.339</a>; administrative services and office space; the review and processing of complaints; the setting of board fees, unless otherwise provided in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.32">326.32</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.339">326.339</a>; and other provisions relating to board operations shall be as provided in chapter 214.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.33.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.33.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Meetings; chair.</span></h2>
          <p>The board shall meet at the times it considers necessary to conduct business ascribed to the board by the provisions of sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.32">326.32</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.339">326.339</a>. The board shall designate one of its members to fill the position of board chair, and that person may remain in the capacity of chair for a term of one year. The board has the option of replacing a board member as chair.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.33.3">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.33.3">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/360/#laws.0.26.0">1987 c 360 s 26</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.33.4">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.33.4">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/360/#laws.0.26.0">1987 c 360 s 26</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.33.5">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 5." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.33.5">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/360/#laws.0.26.0">1987 c 360 s 26</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.33.6">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 6." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.33.6">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Compensation to board members.</span></h2>
          <p>Members of the Board of Private Detective and Protective Agent Services shall receive, in addition to necessary traveling and lodging expenses, a per diem payment as specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/214.09#stat.214.09.3">214.09, subdivision 3</a>, per day for each day actually engaged in board activities, provided, however, members of the board who are state employees will be governed by state rules regarding travel expense and per diem payments.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.326.33.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1974/0/310/#laws.0.5.0">1974 c 310 s 5</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1975/0/136/#laws.0.64.0">1975 c 136 s 64</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1975/0/271/#laws.0.6.0">1975 c 271 s 6</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1976/0/149/#laws.0.54.0">1976 c 149 s 54</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1976/0/222/#laws.0.170.0">1976 c 222 s 170</a>-172; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1980/0/509/#laws.0.128.0">1980 c 509 s 128</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1982/0/424/#laws.0.130.0">1982 c 424 s 130</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1984/0/531/#laws.0.8.0">1984 c 531 s 8</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1984/0/649/#laws.0.1.0">1984 c 649 s 1</a>,5; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/0/444/#laws.0.0.0">1986 c 444</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/360/#laws.0.7.0">1987 c 360 s 7</a>,8; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2000/0/445/#laws.1.3.0">2000 c 445 art 1 s 3</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.326.331"><b>326.331</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/360/#laws.0.26.0">1987 c 360 s 26</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.326.3311">
        <h1 class="shn">326.3311 POWERS AND DUTIES.</h1>
        <p>The board has the following powers and duties:</p>
        <p>(1) to receive and review all applications for private detective and protective agent licenses;</p>
        <p>(2) to approve applications for private detective and protective agent licenses and issue, or reissue licenses as provided in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.32">326.32</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.339">326.339</a>;</p>
        <p>(3) to deny applications for private detective and protective agent licenses if the applicants do not meet the requirements of sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.32">326.32</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.339">326.339</a>; upon denial of a license application, the board shall notify the applicant of the denial and the facts and circumstances that constitute the denial; the board shall advise the applicant of the right to a contested case hearing under chapter 14;</p>
        <p>(4) to enforce all laws and rules governing private detectives and protective agents;</p>
        <p>(5) to suspend or revoke the license of a license holder or impose a civil penalty on a license holder for violations of any provision of sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.32">326.32</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.339">326.339</a> or the rules of the board;</p>
        <p>(6) to investigate and refer for prosecution all criminal violations by individuals and entities; and</p>
        <p>(7) to investigate and refer for prosecution any individuals and entities operating as private detectives or protective agents without a license.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.326.3311.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/360/#laws.0.9.0">1987 c 360 s 9</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/52/#laws.10.4.0">2023 c 52 art 10 s 4</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.326.332"><b>326.332</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/360/#laws.0.26.0">1987 c 360 s 26</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.326.3321">
        <h1 class="shn">326.3321 EMPLOYEES.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.3321.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.3321.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Executive director.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner of public safety shall appoint an executive director to serve in the unclassified service at the pleasure of the commissioner. The executive director shall perform the duties as the board and commissioner shall prescribe.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.3321.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.3321.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Others.</span></h2>
          <p>The board may employ and assign duties to other employees or agents as it considers necessary to discharge the functions of the board.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.326.3321.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/360/#laws.0.10.0">1987 c 360 s 10</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1997/0/239/#laws.8.24.0">1997 c 239 art 8 s 24</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.326.333"><b>326.333</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/360/#laws.0.26.0">1987 c 360 s 26</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.326.3331">
        <h1 class="shn">326.3331 RULEMAKING.</h1>
        <p>The board shall adopt rules under chapter 14 to govern the selection, training, conduct, discipline, and licensing of private detectives and protective agents, and any other matters necessary to carry out duties imposed by sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.32">326.32</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.339">326.339</a>.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.326.3331.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/360/#laws.0.11.0">1987 c 360 s 11</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.326.334"><b>326.334</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/360/#laws.0.26.0">1987 c 360 s 26</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.326.3341">
        <h1 class="shn">326.3341 EXEMPTIONS.</h1>
        <p>Sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.32">326.32</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.339">326.339</a> do not apply to:</p>
        <p>(1) an employee while providing security or conducting an investigation of a pending or potential claim against the employee's employer;</p>
        <p>(2) a peace officer or employee of the United States, this state or one of its political subdivisions, while engaged in the discharge of official duties for the government employer;</p>
        <p>(3) persons engaged exclusively in obtaining and furnishing information as to the financial standing, rating, and credit responsibility of persons or as to the personal habits and financial responsibility of applicants for insurance, indemnity bonds, or commercial credit;</p>
        <p>(4) an attorney-at-law while performing the duties of an attorney-at-law or an investigator employed exclusively by an attorney or a law firm engaged in investigating legal matters;</p>
        <p>(5) a collection agency or finance company licensed to do business under the laws of this state or an employee of one of those companies while acting within the scope of employment when making an investigation incidental to the business of the agency, including an investigation as to location of a debtor, of the debtor's assets or property, provided the client has a financial interest in or a lien upon the assets or property of the debtor;</p>
        <p>(6) an insurance adjuster employed exclusively by an insurance company, or licensed as an adjuster with the state of Minnesota and engaged in the business of adjusting insurance claims;</p>
        <p>(7) persons engaged in responding to alarm signals including, but not limited to, fire alarms, industrial process failure alarms and burglary alarms, for purposes of maintaining, repairing or resetting the alarm, or for opening the premises for law enforcement personnel or responding agents; or</p>
        <p>(8) a certified public accountant or a CPA firm, while performing services regulated under chapter 326A.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.326.3341.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/360/#laws.0.12.0">1987 c 360 s 12</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2013/0/69/#laws.0.1.0">2013 c 69 s 1</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.326.335"><b>326.335</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1974/0/310/#laws.0.12.0">1974 c 310 s 12</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.326.336">
        <h1 class="shn">326.336 EMPLOYEES OF LICENSE HOLDERS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.336.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.336.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Background check.</span></h2>
