          <p>A license holder may employ, in connection with the business of private detective or protective agent, as many unlicensed persons as may be necessary; provided that every license holder is at all times accountable for the good conduct of every person employed. When a license holder hires a person to perform services as a private detective or protective agent, the employer shall submit to the Bureau of Criminal Apprehension a full set of fingerprints of each employee and the written consent of the employee to enable the bureau to determine whether that person has a criminal record. The employee is a conditional employee until the employer receives a report from the bureau that, based on a check of the criminal records maintained by the bureau, the prospective employee has not been convicted in Minnesota of a felony or any offense listed in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.3381#stat.326.3381.3">326.3381, subdivision 3</a>, other than a misdemeanor or gross misdemeanor assault. During the period of conditional employment, the person may not serve as a private detective or protective agent, but may be trained by the employer. The bureau shall immediately forward the fingerprints to the Federal Bureau of Investigation and request the Federal Bureau of Investigation to conduct a criminal history check of each conditional employee. The bureau shall determine if the Federal Bureau of Investigation report indicates that the employee was convicted of a disqualifying offense and shall notify the employer accordingly. The employer shall immediately dismiss an employee who has been convicted of a disqualifying offense.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.336.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.336.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Identification card.</span></h2>
          <p>An identification card must be issued by the license holder to each employee. The card must be in the possession of the employee to whom it is issued at all times. The identification card must contain the license holder's name, logo (if any), address or Minnesota office address, and the employee's photograph and physical description. The card must be signed by the employee and by the license holder, qualified representative, or Minnesota office manager. The card must be presented upon request.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.336.3">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.336.3">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Failure to return property.</span></h2>
          <p>Any person who shall be issued an identification card, badge, holster, weapon, shield, or any other equipment bearing the name, trademark or trade name, or any combination thereof, of any licensed agency, or indicating that such person is a private detective, protective agent, or employee of same, who does not return such badge, weapon, holster, identification card, uniform emblem, or other equipment to the owner thereof within ten days of the termination of employment, or of receiving a written request to return same, made by certified mail to the person's last known address, whichever shall last occur, shall be guilty of a misdemeanor.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.336.4">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.336.4">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Confidentiality; false statements.</span></h2>
          <p>No employee of any license holder shall divulge to anyone other than the employer, or as the employer shall direct, except as may be required by law, any information acquired during such employment in respect of any matter or investigation undertaken or done by such employer. Any employee who shall make any false statement in an employment statement or who willfully makes a false report to the employer in respect to any matter in the course of the employer's business, or who shall otherwise violate the provisions of this subdivision is guilty of a misdemeanor.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.326.336.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1945/0/130/#laws.0.7.0">1945 c 130 s 7</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1959/0/317/#laws.0.5.0">1959 c 317 s 5</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1969/0/1129/#laws.1.3.0">1969 c 1129 art 1 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1974/0/310/#laws.0.7.0">1974 c 310 s 7</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1978/0/674/#laws.0.60.0">1978 c 674 s 60</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1984/0/649/#laws.0.5.0">1984 c 649 s 5</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/0/444/#laws.0.0.0">1986 c 444</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/360/#laws.0.13.0">1987 c 360 s 13</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/171/#laws.0.3.0">1989 c 171 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2002/0/321/#laws.0.4.0">2002 c 321 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/52/#laws.10.5.0">2023 c 52 art 10 s 5</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.326.3361">
        <h1 class="shn">326.3361 TRAINING.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.3361.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.3361.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Rules.</span></h2>
          <p>The board shall, by rule, prescribe the requirements, duration, contents, and standards for successful completion of certified training programs for license holders, qualified representatives, Minnesota managers, partners, and employees, including:</p>
          <p>(1) for those individuals who are armed with a firearm, training in the proper use of, and the risks and dangers arising from the use of, firearms;</p>
          <p>(2) for those individuals who are armed with a weapon, training in the proper use of, and the risks and dangers arising from the use of, weapons other than firearms, including, but not limited to, bludgeons, nightsticks, batons, chemical weapons, and electronic incapacitation devices, and restraint or immobilization techniques;</p>
          <p>(3) for those individuals who are armed with a firearm or armed with a weapon, training in first aid and alternatives to the use of force, including advantages to not using force and specifically when force should not be used;</p>
          <p>(4) for those individuals who are armed with a firearm or armed with a weapon, training in the legal limitations on the justifiable use of force and deadly force as specified in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.06">609.06</a> and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.065">609.065</a>;</p>
          <p>(5) standards for weapons and equipment issued to or carried or used by those individuals;</p>
          <p>(6) preassignment or on-the-job training, or its equivalent, required before applicants may be certified as having completed training; and</p>
          <p>(7) continuing training for license holders, qualified representatives, Minnesota managers, partners, employees, individuals armed with a firearm, and individuals armed with a weapon.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.3361.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.3361.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Required contents.</span></h2>
          <p>The rules adopted by the board must require:</p>
          <p>(1) 12 hours of preassignment or on-the-job certified training within the first 21 days of employment, or evidence that the employee has successfully completed equivalent training before the start of employment. Notwithstanding any statute or rule to the contrary, this clause is satisfied if the employee provides a prospective employer with a certificate or a copy of a certificate demonstrating that the employee successfully completed this training prior to employment with a different Minnesota licensee and completed this training within three previous calendar years, or successfully completed this training with a Minnesota licensee while previously employed with a Minnesota licensee. The certificate or a copy of the certificate is the property of the employee who completed the training, regardless of who paid for the training or how training was provided. Upon a current or former employee's request, a current or former licensed employer must provide a copy of a certificate demonstrating the employee's successful completion of training to the current or former employee. The current or former licensed employer must not charge the employee a fee for a copy of the certificate. The employee who completed the training is entitled to access a copy of the certificate at no charge according to sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/181.960">181.960</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/181.966">181.966</a>. A current or former employer must comply with sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/181.960">181.960</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/181.966">181.966</a>;</p>
          <p>(2) certification by the board of completion of certified training for a license holder, qualified representative, Minnesota manager, partner, and employee to carry or use a firearm, a weapon other than a firearm, or an immobilizing or restraint technique; and</p>
          <p>(3) six hours a year of certified continuing training for all license holders, qualified representatives, Minnesota managers, partners, and employees, and an additional six hours a year for individuals who are armed with firearms or armed with weapons, which must include annual certification of the individual.</p>
          <p>An individual may not carry or use a weapon while undergoing on-the-job training under this subdivision.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.3361.3">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.3361.3">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Use of weapons; certified training required.</span></h2>
          <p>The rules must provide that no license holder, qualified representative, Minnesota manager, partner, or employee may carry or use a weapon or immobilizing or restraint technique without having successfully completed certified training as directed by the board.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.3361.4">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.3361.4">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Full-time peace officers.</span></h2>
          <p>A person licensed as a peace officer by the Board of Peace Officer Standards and Training meets the training requirements of this section.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.326.3361.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1990/0/485/#laws.0.2.0">1990 c 485 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/168/#laws.0.1.0">1993 c 168 s 1</a>-3; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2000/0/445/#laws.1.4.0">2000 c 445 art 1 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2001/0/168/#laws.0.3.0">2001 c 168 s 3</a>,4; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/52/#laws.10.6.0">2023 c 52 art 10 s 6</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.326.337"><b>326.337</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/360/#laws.0.26.0">1987 c 360 s 26</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.326.338">
        <h1 class="shn">326.338 PERSONS ENGAGED AS PRIVATE DETECTIVES OR PROTECTIVE AGENTS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.338.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.338.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Private detective.</span></h2>
          <p>Persons who for a fee, reward, or other consideration, undertake any of the following acts for the purpose of obtaining information for others are considered to be engaged in the business of a private detective:</p>
          <p>(1) investigating crimes or wrongs done or threatened against the government of the United States or of any state, county, or municipal subdivision thereof;</p>
          <p>(2) investigating the identity, habits, conduct, movements, whereabouts, transactions, reputation, or character of any person or organization;</p>
          <p>(3) investigating the credibility of witnesses or other persons;</p>
          <p>(4) investigating the location or recovery of lost or stolen property;</p>
          <p>(5) investigating the origin of and responsibility for libels, losses, accidents, or damage or injuries to persons or property;</p>
          <p>(6) investigating the affiliation, connection, or relationship of any person, firm, or corporation with any organization, society, or association, or with any official, representative, or member thereof;</p>
          <p>(7) investigating the conduct, honesty, efficiency, loyalty, or activities of employees, persons seeking employment, agents, or contractors and subcontractors;</p>
          <p>(8) obtaining through investigation evidence to be used before any authorized investigating committee, board of award, board of arbitration, administrative body, or officer or in preparation for trial of civil or criminal cases; or</p>
          <p>(9) investigating the identity or apprehension of persons suspected of crimes or misdemeanors.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.338.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.338.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/360/#laws.0.26.0">1987 c 360 s 26</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.338.3">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.338.3">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/360/#laws.0.26.0">1987 c 360 s 26</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.338.4">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.338.4">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Protective agent.</span></h2>
          <p>A person who for a fee, reward, or other valuable consideration undertakes any of the following acts is considered to be engaged in the business of protective agent:</p>
          <p>(1) providing guards, private patrol, or other security personnel to protect persons or their property or to prevent the theft, unlawful taking of goods, merchandise, or money, or to prevent the misappropriation or concealment of goods, merchandise, money, or other valuable things, or to procure the return of those things;</p>
          <p>(2) physically responding to any alarm signal device, burglar alarm, television camera, still camera, or a mechanical or electronic device installed or used to prevent or detect burglary, theft, shoplifting, pilferage, losses, or other security measures;</p>
          <p>(3) providing armored car services for the protection of persons or property;</p>
          <p>(4) controlling motor traffic on public streets, roads, and highways for the purpose of escorting a funeral procession and oversized loads;</p>
          <p>(5) providing management and control of crowds for the purpose of safety and protection; or</p>
          <p>(6) providing guards or other security personnel to transport prisoners or any other person arrested on a warrant, except that this does not apply to the transport or escort of offenders by staff of the Department of Corrections; the transport of a person by the sheriff of a county to the appropriate adult or juvenile correctional facility as designated by the commissioner of corrections or to and from court in connection with postconviction, habeas corpus, or intrastate mandatory disposition of detainers proceedings; the transfer of a person by emergency medical services personnel; or the transfer of a person by a peace officer as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/626.84#stat.626.84.1">626.84, subdivision 1</a>, paragraph (c).</p>
          <p>A person covered by this subdivision may perform the traffic-control duties in clause (4) in place of a police officer when a special permit is required, provided that the protective agent is first-aid qualified.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.326.338.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1945/0/130/#laws.0.9.0">1945 c 130 s 9</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1959/0/317/#laws.0.7.0">1959 c 317 s 7</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1974/0/310/#laws.0.9.0">1974 c 310 s 9</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/0/444/#laws.0.0.0">1986 c 444</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/360/#laws.0.14.0">1987 c 360 s 14</a>,15; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1996/0/387/#laws.0.6.0">1996 c 387 s 6</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/123/#laws.5.13.0">2024 c 123 art 5 s 13</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.326.3381">
        <h1 class="shn">326.3381 LICENSES.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.3381.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.3381.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Prohibition.</span></h2>
          <p>No person shall engage in the business of private detective or protective agent, or advertise or indicate in any verbal statement or in written material that the person is so engaged or available to supply those services, without having first obtained a license as provided in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.32">326.32</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.339">326.339</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.3381.1a">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 1a." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.3381.1a">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 1a.<span class="headnote">Proprietary employers.</span></h2>
          <p>A proprietary employer is not required to obtain a license, but must comply with section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.336#stat.326.336.1">326.336, subdivision 1</a>, with respect to the hiring of security guards.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.3381.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.3381.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Application procedure.</span></h2>
          <p>The board shall issue a license upon application to any person qualified under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.32">326.32</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.339">326.339</a> and under the rules of the board to engage in the business of private detective or protective agent. The license shall remain effective for two years as long as the license holder complies with sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.32">326.32</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.339">326.339</a>, the laws of Minnesota, and the rules of the board. Upon receipt of an application for private detective or protective agent license, the board shall:</p>
          <p>(1) post notice of the application in its office for a period of 20 days, and notify all persons who have requested notification of applications;</p>
          <p>(2) conduct an investigation as it considers necessary to determine the qualifications of the applicant, qualified representative, Minnesota manager, and if appropriate, a partner or corporate officer; and</p>
          <p>(3) notify the applicant of the date on which the board will conduct a review of the license application.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.3381.3">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.3381.3">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Disqualification.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) No person is qualified to hold a license who has:</p>
          <p>(1) been convicted of (i) a felony by the courts of this or any other state or of the United States; (ii) acts which, if done in Minnesota, would be criminal sexual conduct; assault; theft; larceny; burglary; robbery; carjacking; unlawful entry; extortion; defamation; buying or receiving stolen property; using, possessing, manufacturing, or carrying weapons unlawfully; using, possessing, or carrying burglary tools unlawfully; escape; possession, production, sale, or distribution of narcotics unlawfully; or (iii) in any other country of acts which, if done in Minnesota, would be a felony or would be any of the other offenses provided in this clause and for which a full pardon or similar relief has not been granted;</p>
          <p>(2) made any false statement in an application for a license or any document required to be submitted to the board; or</p>
          <p>(3) failed to demonstrate to the board good character, honesty, and integrity.</p>
          <p>(b) Upon application for a license, the applicant shall submit, as part of the application, a full set of fingerprints and the applicant's written consent that their fingerprints shall be submitted to the Bureau of Criminal Apprehension (BCA) and the Federal Bureau of Investigation (FBI) to determine whether that person has a criminal record. The BCA shall promptly forward the fingerprints to the FBI and request that the FBI conduct a criminal history check of each prospective licensee. The Minnesota Board of Private Detective and Protective Agents Services shall determine if the FBI report indicates that the prospective licensee or licensee was convicted of a disqualifying offense. The submission to the FBI shall be coordinated through the BCA. The results of the criminal record check shall be provided to the board who will determine if the applicant is disqualified from holding a license under this subdivision.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.3381.4">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.3381.4">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Business entity applicant.</span></h2>
          <p>If the applicant for a license is a corporation or partnership, one member of that corporation or partnership must meet the licensing requirements in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.32">326.32</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.339">326.339</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.3381.5">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 5." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.3381.5">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Nonresident applicant.</span></h2>
          <p>If an applicant's home office is located outside of Minnesota, and the applicant establishes a Minnesota office, the applicant shall provide a manager for the Minnesota office who meets the licensing requirements in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.32">326.32</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.339">326.339</a>.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.326.3381.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/360/#laws.0.16.0">1987 c 360 s 16</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/171/#laws.0.4.0">1989 c 171 s 4</a>,5; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/52/#laws.5.45.0">2023 c 52 art 5 s 45</a>; art 20 s 15</p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.326.3382">
        <h1 class="shn">326.3382 APPLICATION FOR LICENSE.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.3382.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.3382.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Application form.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Application for a private detective or protective agent license shall be made on a form prescribed by the board. Each applicant shall provide the following information:</p>
          <p>(1) the full name, date of birth, and sex of each person signing the application, and the residences of those persons for the past five years;</p>
          <p>(2) all past and present occupations and employers, length of employment, and the name, address, and telephone numbers of supervisors for all persons signing the application;</p>
          <p>(3) the address or a description indicating the location of the place of business of the applicant;</p>
          <p>(4) a statement indicating that each person signing the application has attained the age of 18;</p>
          <p>(5) if the applicant is a corporation, the name of the corporation, the date and place of incorporation, and the location of its principal place of business or registered office in its state of incorporation; and</p>
          <p>(6) further facts as may be required by the board to show the good character, competency, and integrity of each person signing the application; and</p>
          <p>(b) each application shall be signed and acknowledged as follows:</p>
          <p>(1) if the applicant is an individual, by the individual;</p>
          <p>(2) if the applicant is a partnership, by each partner, one of whom must be a qualified representative; or</p>
          <p>(3) if the applicant is a corporation, by the chief executive officer, chief financial officer, and the qualified representative of the corporation. If the principal place of the applicant's business is outside Minnesota, the application shall also include the signature of the Minnesota manager.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.3382.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.3382.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Documents accompanying application.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Each individual signing the application shall submit:</p>
          <p>(1) references, on forms provided by the board, from five persons who have known the signer for at least five years, and who are not related by blood or marriage to the signer; and</p>
          <p>(2) a recent photograph and a full set of fingerprints for each person signing the application.</p>
          <p>(b) If the application is for a private detective license, the individual signing the application shall submit a statement under oath by a present or previous employer that the applicant for an individual license, the qualified representative for a partnership or corporate license, or the Minnesota manager, as appropriate, has been employed as an investigator for a minimum of 6,000 hours by any of the following:</p>
          <p>(1) a licensed private detective agency;</p>
          <p>(2) a United States government investigative service;</p>
          <p>(3) a city police department or sheriff's office; or</p>
          <p>(4) an occupation that, the board finds equivalent in scope, responsibility, and training to one of the specific occupations listed;</p>
          <p class="bl">and has the qualifications established in the rules of the board.</p>
          <p>(c) If the application is for a protective agent license, each person signing the application shall submit a statement under oath by a present or previous employer that the applicant for an individual license, the qualified representative for a partnership or corporate license, or the Minnesota manager has been employed as an investigator or protective agent for a minimum of 6,000 hours by any of the following:</p>
          <p>(1) a licensed protective agent or licensed private detective, having gained experience in security systems, audits, and supervision;</p>
          <p>(2) a United States government investigative service;</p>
          <p>(3) a city police department or sheriff's office; or</p>
          <p>(4) an occupation that the board finds equivalent in scope, responsibility, and training to one of the specific occupations listed;</p>
          <p class="bl">and has the qualifications established in the rules of the board.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.3382.3">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.3382.3">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Proof of insurance.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) No license may be issued to a private detective or protective agent applicant until the applicant has complied with the requirements in this subdivision.</p>
          <p>(b) The applicant shall execute a surety bond to the state of Minnesota in the penal sum of $10,000 and file it with the board. The surety bond must be executed by a company authorized to do business in the state of Minnesota, must name the applicant as principal, and must state that the applicant and each of the applicant's employees shall faithfully observe all of the laws of Minnesota and of the United States and shall pay all damages suffered by any person by reason of a violation of law by the applicant or by the commission of any willful and malicious wrong by the applicant in the course of business.</p>
          <p>(c) The applicant shall furnish proof, acceptable to the board, of the applicant's ability to respond in damages for liability on account of accidents or wrongdoings arising out of the ownership and operation of a private detective or protective agent business. Compliance with paragraph (d), (e), or (f) is satisfactory proof of financial responsibility for purposes of this paragraph.</p>
          <p>(d) The applicant may file with the board a certificate of insurance demonstrating coverage for general liability, completed operations, and personal injury. Personal injury insurance must include coverage for:</p>
          <p>(1) false arrest, detention, imprisonment, and malicious prosecution;</p>
          <p>(2) libel, slander, defamation, and violation of rights of privacy; and</p>
          <p>(3) wrongful entry, eviction, and other invasion of rights of private occupancy.</p>
          <p>In the event of a policy cancellation, the insurer will send notice to the board at the same time that a cancellation request is received from or a notice is sent to the insured.</p>
          <p>(e) The applicant may file with the board an annual net worth statement, signed by a licensed certified public accountant, evidencing that the applicant has a net worth of at least the following:</p>
          <p>(1) for an applicant with no employees, $10,000;</p>
          <p>(2) for an applicant with one to ten employees, $15,000;</p>
          <p>(3) for an applicant with 11 to 25 employees, $25,000;</p>
          <p>(4) for an applicant with 26 to 50 employees, $50,000; or</p>
          <p>(5) for an applicant with 51 or more employees, $100,000.</p>
          <p>Data indicating with which of the above requirements an applicant must comply is public data. The contents of the net worth statement are private data on individuals or nonpublic data, as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/13.02">13.02</a>.</p>
          <p>(f) The applicant may file with the board an irrevocable letter of credit from a financial institution acceptable to the board in the amount listed in the appropriate category in paragraph (e).</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.3382.4">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.3382.4">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">License disqualification.</span></h2>
          <p>Unlicensed activity will not be considered as legitimate experience for qualification in being licensed. An individual, partnership, corporation, qualified representative, or Minnesota manager engaged in the business of a private detective or protective agent without a license issued by the board is prohibited from applying for licensing for a period of one year from the date of a finding of the violation.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.3382.5">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 5." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.3382.5">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Special protective agent classification.</span></h2>
          <p>The board shall establish a special protective agent license classification that provides that a person described in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.338#stat.326.338.4">326.338, subdivision 4</a>, clause (4), who is otherwise qualified under this section need not meet the requirements of subdivision 2, paragraph (c).</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.3382.6">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 6." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.3382.6">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Temporary military license.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">The board shall establish a temporary license to engage in the business of private detective or protective agent in accordance with section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/197.4552">197.4552</a>. The fee for the temporary license under this subdivision for a private detective is $1,000. The fee for a temporary license under this subdivision for a protective agent is $800.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.326.3382.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/360/#laws.0.17.0">1987 c 360 s 17</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/0/136/#laws.8.11.0">2005 c 136 art 8 s 11</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2010/0/384/#laws.0.85.0">2010 c 384 s 85</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/312/#laws.4.21.0">2014 c 312 art 4 s 21</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.326.3383">
        <h1 class="shn">326.3383 LICENSE REISSUANCE.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.3383.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.3383.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Requirements.</span></h2>
          <p>The board shall reissue a private detective or protective agent license to a license holder without further board review, if the license holder who has complied with all applicable laws and rules:</p>
          <p>(1) submits to the board an application for license reissuance on a form prescribed by the board;</p>
          <p>(2) submits to the board a list of all current employees; and</p>
          <p>(3) remits the expired license to the board.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.3383.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.3383.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Appearance.</span></h2>
          <p>Nothing in this section shall preclude the board from requiring the appearance of the license holder at a board meeting prior to the reissuance of the license.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.3383.3">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.3383.3">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Bond and proof of financial responsibility.</span></h2>
          <p>Each applicant for license reissuance shall maintain a $10,000 surety bond, and show proof of financial responsibility as required in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.3382#stat.326.3382.3">326.3382, subdivision 3</a>.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.326.3383.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/360/#laws.0.18.0">1987 c 360 s 18</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.326.3384">
        <h1 class="shn">326.3384 PROHIBITED ACTS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.3384.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.3384.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Prohibition.</span></h2>
          <p>No license holder or employee of a license holder shall, in a manner that implies that the person is an employee or agent of a governmental agency, display on a badge, identification card, emblem, vehicle, uniform, stationery, or in advertising for private detective or protective agent services:</p>
          <p>(1) the words "public safety," "police," "highway patrol," "state patrol," "sheriff," "trooper," or "law enforcement"; or</p>
          <p>(2) the name of a municipality, county, state, or of the United States, or any governmental subdivision thereof.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.3384.1a">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 1a." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.3384.1a">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 1a.<span class="headnote">Labor disputes.</span></h2>
          <p>No license holder, in the course of providing protective agent services, may provide armed protective personnel to labor disputes or strike locations. This subdivision does not apply to the use of armed security personnel services utilized in the usual course of business for the protection of persons, property, and payroll.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.3384.1b">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 1b." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.3384.1b">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 1b.<span class="headnote">Acts prohibited during labor disputes, strikes, and lockouts.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) This subdivision applies to (1) a license holder or an employee of a license holder who is primarily performing the duties of a protective agent; or (2) a security guard who is primarily performing the duties of a security guard.</p>
          <p>(b) A person described in paragraph (a) is prohibited from doing any of the activities described in clauses (1) to (5) during a labor dispute, strike, or lockout as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/179.01">179.01, subdivisions 7, 8, and 9</a>:</p>
          <p>(1) inciting, encouraging, or aiding in the incitement or encouragement of any participant to do unlawful acts against the person or property of anyone;</p>
          <p>(2) photographing a participant when neither that person nor the photographer is on the premises being protected by the persons described in paragraph (a);</p>
          <p>(3) stopping or detaining any vehicle unless the vehicle is on premises being protected by the persons described in paragraph (a);</p>
          <p>(4) conducting surveillance of participants, when neither the participant nor the person conducting the surveillance is on the premises being protected by the person described in paragraph (a), or of their businesses, or homes; or</p>
          <p>(5) any other activities that are outside of the scope of the duties described in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.32#stat.326.32.13">326.32, subdivision 13</a>, and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.338#stat.326.338.4">326.338, subdivision 4</a>, and have the purpose of intimidating or provoking a participant.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.3384.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.3384.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Penalty.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) A person violating this section is guilty of a gross misdemeanor.</p>
          <p>(b) The board shall suspend the license of a license holder for the periods described in paragraph (c) if the license holder or an employee of the license holder is convicted of a violation of subdivision 1b. The board shall prohibit an employee of a license holder from working for any license holder for the periods described in paragraph (c) if the employee is convicted of a violation of subdivision 1b.</p>
          <p>(c) The periods described in paragraph (b) are as follows:</p>
          <p>(1) 60 days for the first violation;</p>
          <p>(2) six months for the second violation; and</p>
          <p>(3) one year for the third violation.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.326.3384.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/360/#laws.0.19.0">1987 c 360 s 19</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/171/#laws.0.6.0">1989 c 171 s 6</a>,7; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1990/0/485/#laws.0.3.0">1990 c 485 s 3</a>,4; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/0/10/#laws.2.4.0">2005 c 10 art 2 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/0/136/#laws.11.16.0">2005 c 136 art 11 s 16</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.326.3385">
        <h1 class="shn">326.3385 CONDITIONS OF LICENSING.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.3385.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.3385.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Notice of address change.</span></h2>
          <p>A license holder who moves to an address other than that given on the license certificate shall give written notice to the board within seven days of the move. The notice shall give the new address or location, the date the move was made, and be accompanied by the license, at which time a new license will be made showing the new address or location.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.3385.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.3385.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Notice of successor.</span></h2>
          <p>A corporate or partnership license holder shall, within seven days of the death, resignation, or removal of a person signing the license application, give written notice to the board of the change and the name and address of the successor in the vacated position.</p>
          <p>Within seven days of the death, resignation, or removal of a person signing the license application for a partnership or corporate license holder, the successor qualified representative, partner, Minnesota manager, chief executive officer, or chief financial officer who shall qualify under the same procedure and criteria, and submit the documents required, as for an original application.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.3385.3">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.3385.3">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Surrender of license.</span></h2>
          <p>Every license issued to a license holder shall be surrendered to the board within seven days after its expiration, or upon notice to a license holder that a license has been revoked or suspended. If the license cannot be returned, a notarized statement indicating the circumstances shall be submitted to the board.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.3385.4">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.3385.4">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Penalty.</span></h2>
          <p>Failure to comply with the provisions of subdivision 1, 2, or 3 may result in the revocation or suspension of the license, or the imposition of an administrative penalty.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.326.3385.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/360/#laws.0.20.0">1987 c 360 s 20</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.326.3386">
        <h1 class="shn">326.3386 FEES.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.3386.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.3386.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Application fee.</span></h2>
          <p>Each applicant for a private detective or protective agent license shall pay to the board a nonrefundable application fee, as determined by the board.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.3386.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.3386.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">License fee.</span></h2>
          <p>Each applicant for a private detective or protective agent license shall pay to the board a license fee, as determined by the board. In the event that an applicant is denied licensing by the board, one-half of the license fee shall be refunded to the applicant.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.3386.3">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.3386.3">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Designation fee.</span></h2>
          <p>When a licensed private detective or protective agent who is a partnership or corporation, desires to designate a new qualified representative or Minnesota manager, a fee equal to one-half of the license fee shall be submitted to the board.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.3386.4">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.3386.4">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Status fee.</span></h2>
          <p>At the time a licensed private detective or protective agent wishes to change a license status, as in the case of an individual license holder establishing a corporation, the difference between the individual license fee and the corporate license fee shall be paid to the board.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.3386.5">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 5." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.3386.5">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Reissuance fee.</span></h2>
          <p>License holders seeking license reissuance shall pay to the board a license reissuance fee as determined by the board.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.3386.6">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 6." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.3386.6">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Business or division fee.</span></h2>
          <p>If a private detective or protective agent license holder wishes to add additional business names or corporate division names to an existing license, the license holder shall be required to pay a fee as determined by the board.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.3386.6a">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 6a." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.3386.6a">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6a.<span class="headnote">Training course certification fee.</span></h2>
          <p>An applicant for training course certification, as specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.3361">326.3361</a>, shall pay to the board a course certification fee determined by the board.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.3386.6b">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 6b." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.3386.6b">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6b.<span class="headnote">Training course recertification fee.</span></h2>
          <p>An applicant for training course recertification shall pay to the board a course recertification fee determined by the board.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.3386.7">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 7." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.3386.7">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Rules.</span></h2>
          <p>All fees authorized by this section shall be established by rule by the board. All fees paid to the board shall be paid to the general fund. The cost of administering sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.32">326.32</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.339">326.339</a>, shall be paid from appropriations made to the board.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.326.3386.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/360/#laws.0.21.0">1987 c 360 s 21</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1997/0/239/#laws.8.25.0">1997 c 239 art 8 s 25</a>-27</p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.326.3387">
        <h1 class="shn">326.3387 DISCIPLINARY ACTION.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.3387.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.3387.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Basis for action.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The board may revoke or suspend or refuse to issue or reissue a private detective or protective agent license if:</p>
          <p>(1) the license holder violates a provision of sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.32">326.32</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.339">326.339</a> or a rule adopted under those sections;</p>
          <p>(2) the license holder has engaged in fraud, deceit, or misrepresentation while in the business of private detective or protective agent;</p>
          <p>(3) the license holder has made a false statement in an application submitted to the board or in a document required to be submitted to the board;</p>
          <p>(4) the license holder violates an order of the board; or</p>
          <p>(5) the individual or entity previously operated without a license.</p>
          <p>(b) The board must revoke or suspend or refuse to issue or reissue a protective agent license if the license holder provides guards or other security personnel to transport prisoners or any other person arrested on a warrant and the board determines that the license holder or any employee or agent of the license holder committed an act in any place that, if committed in Minnesota, would constitute criminal sexual conduct against a person being transported or committed an act in any place that involved the unreasonable use of force on a person being transported.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.3387.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.3387.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Hearing required.</span></h2>
          <p>The board may impose the following penalties only after a contested case hearing under chapter 14:</p>
          <p>(1) revoke or suspend a private detective or protective agent license; or</p>
          <p>(2) impose an administrative penalty in excess of $500.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.326.3387.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/360/#laws.0.22.0">1987 c 360 s 22</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/52/#laws.10.7.0">2023 c 52 art 10 s 7</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/123/#laws.5.14.0">2024 c 123 art 5 s 14</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.326.3388">
        <h1 class="shn">326.3388 ADMINISTRATIVE PENALTIES.</h1>
        <p>The board shall, by rule, establish a graduated schedule of administrative penalties for violations of sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.32">326.32</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.339">326.339</a> or the board's rules. The schedule must include minimum and maximum penalties for each violation and be based on and reflect the culpability, frequency, and severity of the violator's actions. The minimum penalty for an act described in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.3387#stat.326.3387.1">326.3387, subdivision 1</a>, paragraph (b), must be $10,000 for each act. The board may impose a penalty from the schedule on a license holder for a violation of sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.32">326.32</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.339">326.339</a> or the rules of the board. The penalty is in addition to any criminal penalty imposed for the same violation. Administrative penalties imposed by the board must be paid to the general fund.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.326.3388.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/360/#laws.0.23.0">1987 c 360 s 23</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/123/#laws.5.15.0">2024 c 123 art 5 s 15</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.326.3389">
        <h1 class="shn">326.3389 LICENSES NONTRANSFERABLE.</h1>
        <p>A license issued under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.32">326.32</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.339">326.339</a> may not be transferred.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.326.3389.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/360/#laws.0.24.0">1987 c 360 s 24</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.326.339">
        <h1 class="shn">326.339 VIOLATIONS; PENALTY.</h1>
        <p>Unless otherwise specifically provided any violation of any provision or requirement of sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.32">326.32</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.339">326.339</a> is a gross misdemeanor.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.326.339.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1945/0/130/#laws.0.10.0">1945 c 130 s 10</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1974/0/310/#laws.0.10.0">1974 c 310 s 10</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/360/#laws.0.25.0">1987 c 360 s 25</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.326.34"><b>326.34</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1945/0/130/#laws.0.1.0">1945 c 130 s 1</a>]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.326.35"><b>326.35</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1945/0/130/#laws.0.1.0">1945 c 130 s 1</a>]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.326.36"><b>326.36</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1945/0/130/#laws.0.1.0">1945 c 130 s 1</a>]</div>
      <div class="sr_by_subd" id="stat.326.37">
        <h1>326.37 </h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.37.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.37.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.</h2>
          <p>MS 2006 [Renumbered <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326B.43#stat.326B.43.1">326B.43, subdivision 1</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.37.1a">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 1a." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.37.1a">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 1a.</h2>
          <p>MS 2006 [Renumbered <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326B.43">326B.43, subd 2</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.37.1b">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 1b." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.37.1b">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 1b.</h2>
          <p>MS 2006 [Renumbered <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326B.43">326B.43, subd 3</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.37.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.37.2">§</a>

      <div class="section" id="stat.326.523">
        <h1 class="shn">326.523 LICENSE PROVISIONS; DISTRIBUTION; CANCELLATION.</h1>
        <p>No distributor shall hereafter license feature motion picture films to an exhibitor to be exhibited, shown or performed in this state unless the license provides:</p>
        <p>(1) that all the feature motion picture films, which such distributor will license during the exhibition season, or the unexpired portion thereof, shall be included: the term "all the feature motion picture films" applies to each producer for whom the distributor is acting; and</p>
        <p>(2) that the exhibitor shall have the right to cancel a minimum of 20 percent of the total number of feature motion pictures included in such license where the exhibitor deems the same injurious and damaging to business or offensive on moral, religious, or racial grounds.</p>
        <p>The cancellation shall be made proportionately among the several price brackets, if there be such price brackets in the license agreement. Any number of cancellations to which an exhibitor is entitled, may be made the lowest price bracket at the exhibitor's option.</p>
        <p>The right to cancellation shall not be effective, unless the exhibitor exercises such right by giving notice thereof, to the distributor, by certified mail, within 15 days after being notified of the availability of a feature motion picture. In determining the number of feature motion pictures that may be canceled, fractions of one-half or more shall be counted as one and fractions of less than one-half shall not be counted.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.326.523.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1941/0/460/#laws.0.2.0">1941 c 460 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1978/0/674/#laws.0.60.0">1978 c 674 s 60</a>; 1986 c 444</p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.326.524">
        <h1 class="shn">326.524 LICENSES MAY NOT CONTAIN CERTAIN RESTRICTIONS.</h1>
        <p>No distributor shall license feature motion picture films to an exhibitor to be exhibited, shown, or performed in this state, upon the condition that the exhibitor must also license short subjects, newsreels, trailers, serials, reissue, foreign, and western motion picture films.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.326.524.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1941/0/460/#laws.0.3.0">1941 c 460 s 3</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.326.525">
        <h1 class="shn">326.525 LICENSES, WHEN VOID.</h1>
        <p>Any provision of any license hereafter made and entered into which is contrary to any provisions of sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.523">326.523</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.526">326.526</a> and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.01">326.01</a>, subdivisions 14 to 19, is hereby declared to be against public policy and void.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.326.525.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1941/0/460/#laws.0.4.0">1941 c 460 s 4</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.326.526">
        <h1 class="shn">326.526 APPLICATION OF SECTIONS 326.523 TO 326.526.</h1>
        <p>The provisions of sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.523">326.523</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.526">326.526</a> and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.01">326.01</a>, subdivisions 14 to 19, shall not apply to the licensing of motion picture films to any school, college, university, church, or any educational, fraternal, or religious organizations in this state.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.326.526.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1941/0/460/#laws.0.7.0">1941 c 460 s 7</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.326.53">
        <h1 class="shn">326.53 VIOLATIONS; PENALTY PROVISIONS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.53.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.53.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Generally.</span></h2>
          <p>(1) Any violation of chapter 326A is a gross misdemeanor.</p>
          <p>(2) Every person violating any of the provisions of sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.523">326.523</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.526">326.526</a>, or assisting in such violation, shall, upon conviction thereof, be punished by a fine not exceeding $3,000 or, in default of the payment of such fine, by imprisonment in the county jail for not more than 364 days. In the case of a corporation, the violation of these sections shall be deemed to be also that of the individual directors, officers, or agents of such corporation who have assisted in such violation, or who have authorized, ordered, or done the acts or omissions constituting, in whole or in part, such violation; and, upon conviction thereof, any such directors, officers, or agents shall be punished by fine or imprisonment as herein provided.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.53.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.53.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/206/#laws.0.25.0">1993 c 206 s 25</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.53.3">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.53.3">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.</h2>
          <p>[Renumbered <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.547">326.547</a>]</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.326.53.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">(<a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1940A/cite/35">5697-12</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1940A/cite/35">5705</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1940A/cite/35">5886</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1940A/cite/35">5887-27</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1940A/cite/35">5887-30i</a>) <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1907/0/457/#laws.0.7.0">1907 c 457 s 7</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1909/0/439/#laws.0.8.0">1909 c 439 s 8</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1921/0/523/#laws.0.12.0">1921 c 523 s 12</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1933/0/349/#laws.0.9.0">1933 c 349 s 9</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1933/0/404/#laws.0.3.0">1933 c 404 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1937/0/367/#laws.0.10.0">1937 c 367 s 10</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1941/0/460/#laws.0.5.0">1941 c 460 s 5</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1945/0/380/#laws.0.9.0">1945 c 380 s 9</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1967/1/1/#laws.0.6.0">Ex1967 c 1 s 6</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1977/0/305/#laws.0.45.0">1977 c 305 s 45</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1984/0/628/#laws.3.11.0">1984 c 628 art 3 s 11</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1992/0/542/#laws.0.4.0">1992 c 542 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2001/0/109/#laws.2.5.0">2001 c 109 art 2 s 5</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/52/#laws.6.16.0">2023 c 52 art 6 s 16</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.326.54"><b>326.54</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1983/0/293/#laws.0.115.0">1983 c 293 s 115</a>]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.326.541"><b>326.541</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1983/0/293/#laws.0.115.0">1983 c 293 s 115</a>]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.326.542"><b>326.542</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1983/0/293/#laws.0.115.0">1983 c 293 s 115</a>]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.326.543"><b>326.543</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1983/0/293/#laws.0.115.0">1983 c 293 s 115</a>]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.326.544"><b>326.544</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1983/0/293/#laws.0.115.0">1983 c 293 s 115</a>]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.326.545"><b>326.545</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1983/0/293/#laws.0.115.0">1983 c 293 s 115</a>]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.326.546"><b>326.546</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1983/0/293/#laws.0.115.0">1983 c 293 s 115</a>]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.326.547"><b>326.547</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1983/0/293/#laws.0.115.0">1983 c 293 s 115</a>]</div>
      <h1 class="fgh">MEMBERS OF ARMED FORCES</h1>
      <div class="section" id="stat.326.55">
        <h1 class="shn">326.55 NONPAYMENT OF LICENSE FEES.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.55.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.55.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Definitions.</span></h2>
          <p>As used in this section:</p>
          <p>(1) "Employment essential to the prosecution of the present war and to the national defense" means employment by the United States of America, any of its agencies, or any contractor under the United States of America, or subcontractor under such contractor, in work connected with the prosecution of the present war or for the defense of the United States of America and others of the United Nations during such war.</p>
          <p>(2) "Outside of the United States" means outside of the territorial limits of the 50 states of the United States and the District of Columbia.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.55.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.55.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Members of armed forces need not pay license fees.</span></h2>
          <p>Any person required by law to be licensed or registered in order to carry on or practice a trade, employment, occupation or profession in the state of Minnesota who is also required by law to renew the license or certificate of registration at stated intervals and to pay a fee for such renewal on or before a specified date, or be subject to revocation of the license or certificate or other penalties, who has since the enactment by the Congress of the United States of the Selective Service and Training Act of 1940 entered, or shall hereafter enter, the armed forces of the United States of America, or who has since the enactment of said act been engaged, or shall hereafter be engaged, in employment, outside of the United States, essential to the prosecution of the present war and to the national defense, whose license or certificate of registration was effective at the time of entry into the armed forces or engagement in the employment aforesaid, is hereby exempted from the payment of all renewal fees and from the filing of any application for renewal, which but for this section would have been required as a condition of the renewal of the license or certificate, during the time the person has been in such armed forces or in such employment, and from any penalties for nonpayment or late payment, and is hereby exempted from further payment of such renewal fees and from the making of any application for renewal during the period the person shall remain in such armed forces or is engaged in such employment, and for a further period of six months from discharge from the armed forces, if a member thereof, or from the date of return within the boundaries of the United States if engaged in the employment hereinbefore referred to. The license or certificate in the meantime shall remain in full force and effect, and if it has been canceled or revoked since the date of the enactment of the Selective Service and Training Act of 1940 solely on the ground of nonpayment of renewal fees, or failure to apply for a renewal, it shall be reinstated upon the application of the licensee or registrant or any one on the licensee's or registrant's behalf without the payment of any penalties or costs. Any such person may within six months from the date of release from the armed forces of the United States, if the person has been a member of such armed forces, or from the date of return within the boundaries of the United States if the person has been engaged in employment hereinbefore referred to, make application for a renewal of the license or certificate without penalty and in the same manner as if the person had made application therefor at the time or times specified by existing laws.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.326.55.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1943/0/121/#laws.0.0.0">1943 c 121</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1965/0/45/#laws.0.52.0">1965 c 45 s 52</a>; 1986 c 444</p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.326.56">
        <h1 class="shn">326.56 LICENSES, CERTIFICATES OF REGISTRATION; RENEWALS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.56.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.56.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Definitions.</span></h2>
          <p>For the purposes of this section the terms defined in this subdivision shall have the meanings ascribed to them.</p>
          <p>(1) "Active military service" has the meaning given in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/190.05">190.05</a>, subdivision 5.</p>
          <p>(2) "Employment essential to the prosecution of a war or to the national defense" means employment by the federal government of the United States of America, or any of its agencies, or by a federal government contractor or subcontractor in work connected with the prosecution of a war or for the defense of the United States or its allies.</p>
          <p>(3) "Outside of the United States" means outside of the territorial limits of the 50 states of the United States and the District of Columbia.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.56.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.56.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">License or registration, renewal; exemption for military service.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) Notwithstanding any other provision of statutes, any person required by law to be licensed or registered by the state of Minnesota in order to carry on or practice a trade, employment, occupation or profession within this state who is also required by law to renew the license or certificate of registration at stated intervals and to pay a fee for such renewal on or before a specified date, or be subject to revocation of the license or certificate or other penalties, and who has been ordered into active military service, or who has been engaged, or shall hereafter be engaged, in employment outside of the United States essential to the prosecution of a war or to the national defense, whose license or certificate of registration was effective at the time of the person's entry into active military service or engagement in the employment, is hereby exempted from the payment of all renewal fees and from the filing of any application for renewal, which but for this section would have been required as a condition of the renewal of the license or certificate, during the time the person has been in active military service or that employment, and from any penalties for nonpayment or late payment, and is hereby exempted from further payment of such renewal fees and from the making of any application for renewal during the period the person shall remain in active military service or the employment, and for six months thereafter. The license or certificate in the meantime shall remain in full force and effect, and if it has been canceled or revoked solely on the basis of nonpayment of renewal fees or failure to apply for a renewal, it shall be reinstated upon the application of the licensee or registrant or by anyone on the person's behalf without the payment of any penalties or costs. Any such person may within six months from the date of release from active military service, or from the date of return within the boundaries of the United States if the person has been engaged in the essential employment, make application for a renewal of the license or certificate without penalty and in the same manner as if the person had made application therefor at the time or time specified by existing laws, irrespective of whether the license or certificate has expired or is due to expire within that time period.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) The phrase "license or certificate of registration" in paragraph (a) includes, but is not limited to, a license or certificate of registration for a trade, employment, occupation, or profession for which the state requires firearms and use of force training. Any compensatory job-related education or training considered necessary by the licensee's or registrant's employer must be provided and paid for by the employer and must not be permitted to delay the licensee's or registrant's reemployment.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.326.56.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1951/0/301/#laws.0.1.0">1951 c 301 s 1</a>, 2; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1965/0/45/#laws.0.53.0">1965 c 45 s 53</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/0/444/#laws.0.0.0">1986 c 444</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2006/0/273/#laws.0.10.0">2006 c 273 s 10</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/51/#laws.0.1.0">2007 c 51 s 1</a></p>
      </div>
