      <div class="sr" id="stat.326.66"><b>326.66</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1988/0/629/#laws.0.64.0">1988 c 629 s 64</a>]</div>
      <h1 class="fgh">ASBESTOS ABATEMENT ACT</h1>
      <div class="section" id="stat.326.70">
        <h1 class="shn">326.70 TITLE.</h1>
        <p>Sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.70">326.70</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.81">326.81</a> may be cited as the "Asbestos Abatement Act."</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.326.70.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/303/#laws.0.1.0">1987 c 303 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1990/0/594/#laws.3.16.0">1990 c 594 art 3 s 16</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.326.71">
        <h1 class="shn">326.71 DEFINITIONS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.71.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.71.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Applicability.</span></h2>
          <p>The definitions in this section apply to sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.70">326.70</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.81">326.81</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.71.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.71.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Asbestos.</span></h2>
          <p>"Asbestos" means the asbestiform varieties of chrysotile (serpentine), crocidolite (riebeckite), amosite (cummingtonite-grunerite), anthophyllite, tremolite, and actinolite.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.71.3">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.71.3">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Asbestos-containing material.</span></h2>
          <p>"Asbestos-containing material" means material that contains more than one percent asbestos by microscopic visual estimation by area.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.71.4">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.71.4">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Asbestos-related work.</span></h2>
          <p>"Asbestos-related work" means the enclosure, removal, or encapsulation of asbestos-containing material in a quantity that meets or exceeds 260 linear feet of friable asbestos-containing material on pipes, 160 square feet of friable asbestos-containing material on other facility components, or, if linear feet or square feet cannot be measured, a total of 35 cubic feet of friable asbestos-containing material on or off all facility components in one facility. In the case of single or multifamily residences, "asbestos-related work" also means the enclosure, removal, or encapsulation of greater than ten but less than 260 linear feet of friable asbestos-containing material on pipes, greater than six but less than 160 square feet of friable asbestos-containing material on other facility components, or, if linear feet or square feet cannot be measured, greater than one cubic foot but less than 35 cubic feet of friable asbestos-containing material on or off all facility components in one facility. Asbestos-related work includes asbestos abatement area preparation; enclosure, removal, or encapsulation operations; and an air quality monitoring specified in rule to assure that the abatement and adjacent areas are not contaminated with asbestos fibers during the project and after completion.</p>
          <p>For purposes of this subdivision, the quantity of asbestos-containing material applies separately for every project.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.71.4a">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4a." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.71.4a">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4a.<span class="headnote">Asbestos inspector.</span></h2>
          <p>"Asbestos inspector" means an individual who inspects a site for the presence and condition of asbestos-containing material, or who reinspects a site to assess the condition of previously identified asbestos-containing material or the presence of other asbestos-containing material.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.71.4b">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4b." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.71.4b">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4b.<span class="headnote">Asbestos management activity.</span></h2>
          <p>"Asbestos management activity" means the performance of periodic inspections to determine the existence and condition of asbestos-containing material, the development of site specific written programs for the maintenance of asbestos-containing material in a condition which prevents the release of asbestos fibers, the development of site specific written programs governing response procedures in the event of an asbestos fiber release episode, and the development of project specifications for asbestos-related work projects.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.71.4c">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4c." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.71.4c">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4c.<span class="headnote">Asbestos management planner.</span></h2>
          <p>"Asbestos management planner" means an individual who develops a written site specific asbestos-containing material maintenance plan and a written site specific asbestos fiber release episode response plan addressing asbestos-containing material at the site.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.71.4d">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4d." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.71.4d">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4d.<span class="headnote">Asbestos project designer.</span></h2>
          <p>"Asbestos project designer" means an individual who designs the asbestos-related work project specifications.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.71.5">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 5." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.71.5">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Commissioner.</span></h2>
          <p>"Commissioner" means the commissioner of health.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.71.6">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 6." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.71.6">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Contracting entity.</span></h2>
          <p>"Contracting entity" means a public or private body, board, natural person, corporation, partnership, proprietorship, joint venture, fund, authority, or similar entity that contracts with a person to do asbestos-related work or asbestos management activity for the benefit of the contracting entity.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.71.7">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 7." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.71.7">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/303/#laws.0.21.0">1993 c 303 s 21</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.71.8">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 8." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.71.8">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.<span class="headnote">Person.</span></h2>
          <p>"Person" means an individual, body, board, corporation, partnership, proprietorship, joint venture, fund, authority, or similar entity. This term also applies to the state, its political subdivisions, and any boards, commissions, schools, institutions, or authorities created or recognized by them.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.326.71.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/303/#laws.0.2.0">1987 c 303 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1990/0/594/#laws.3.16.0">1990 c 594 art 3 s 16</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/303/#laws.0.1.0">1993 c 303 s 1</a>-9; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1994/0/567/#laws.0.19.0">1994 c 567 s 19</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/0/165/#laws.0.12.0">1995 c 165 s 12</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/0/185/#laws.0.6.0">1995 c 185 s 6</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1997/0/205/#laws.0.32.0">1997 c 205 s 32</a>,33; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1997/1/5/#laws.0.1.0">1Sp1997 c 5 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2021/0/30/#laws.3.44.0">2021 c 30 art 3 s 44</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.326.72">
        <h1 class="shn">326.72 ASBESTOS LICENSE.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.72.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.72.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">When license required.</span></h2>
          <p>A person within the state intending to directly perform or cause to be performed through subcontracting or similar delegation any asbestos-related work either for financial gain or with respect to the person's own property shall first apply for and obtain a license from the commissioner. The license shall be in writing, be dated when issued, contain an expiration date, be signed by the commissioner, and give the name and address of the person to whom it is issued.</p>
          <p>The domiciled owner of a single family residence is not required to hold a license or pay a project permit fee to conduct asbestos-related work in the domiciled residence.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.72.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.72.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Display of license.</span></h2>
          <p>Licensees shall post a project permit, obtained from the commissioner after compliance with the provisions of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.74">326.74</a> and rules promulgated under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.78">326.78</a>, in a conspicuous place outside of the asbestos work area. The actual license or a copy shall be readily available at the work site for inspection by the commissioner, other public officials charged with the health, safety, and welfare of the state's citizens, and the contracting entity.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.326.72.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/303/#laws.0.3.0">1987 c 303 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/303/#laws.0.10.0">1993 c 303 s 10</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1997/0/205/#laws.0.34.0">1997 c 205 s 34</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.326.73">
        <h1 class="shn">326.73 ASBESTOS CERTIFICATIONS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.73.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.73.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Asbestos-related work certification.</span></h2>
          <p>Before an individual performs asbestos-related work, the individual shall first obtain a certificate from the commissioner certifying that the individual is qualified to perform the work. No certificate shall be issued unless the individual has shown evidence of training or experience in the general commercial construction trades, has taken a course of training in asbestos control and removal, passed an examination in those subjects, and demonstrated to the commissioner the ability to perform asbestos-related work safely in accordance with the current state-of-the-art technology. The commissioner shall specify the course of training necessary. The certificate issued by the commissioner shall be in writing, be dated when issued, contain an expiration date, be signed by the commissioner, and contain the name and address of the individual to whom it is issued. The certificate shall be carried by the individual and be readily available for inspection by the commissioner, other public officials charged with the health, safety, and welfare of the state's citizens, and the contracting entity.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.73.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.73.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Asbestos inspector certification.</span></h2>
          <p>Before an individual performs an asbestos inspection, the individual shall first obtain a certificate from the commissioner. The commissioner shall issue an asbestos inspector certificate to an individual who has shown evidence of completion of training on asbestos inspection specified by the commissioner in rule, passed an examination in that subject, and has shown evidence of experience as required by rule. The certificate issued by the commissioner shall be in writing, be dated when issued, contain an expiration date, be signed by the commissioner, and contain the name and address of the individual to whom it is issued.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.73.3">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.73.3">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Asbestos management planner certification.</span></h2>
          <p>Before an individual develops an asbestos management plan, the individual shall first obtain a certificate from the commissioner. The commissioner shall issue an asbestos management planner certificate to an individual who has shown evidence of completion of training on asbestos management plan development specified by the commissioner in rule, passed an examination in that subject, and has shown evidence of experience as required by rule. The certificate issued by the commissioner shall be in writing, be dated when issued, contain an expiration date, be signed by the commissioner, and contain the name and address of the individual to whom it is issued.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.73.4">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.73.4">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Asbestos project designer certification.</span></h2>
          <p>Before an individual designs an asbestos-related work project, the individual shall first obtain a certificate from the commissioner. The commissioner shall issue an asbestos project designer certificate to an individual who has shown evidence of completion of training on asbestos project design specified by the commissioner in rule, passed an examination in that subject, and has shown evidence of experience as required by rule. The certificate issued by the commissioner shall be in writing, be dated when issued, contain an expiration date, be signed by the commissioner, and contain the name and address of the individual to whom it is issued.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.326.73.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/303/#laws.0.4.0">1987 c 303 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1988/0/689/#laws.2.233.0">1988 c 689 art 2 s 233</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/303/#laws.0.11.0">1993 c 303 s 11</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.326.74">
        <h1 class="shn">326.74 REPORTING ASBESTOS WORK.</h1>
        <p>Written notice shall be given to the commissioner of an asbestos-related work project by the person holding the license issued under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.72#stat.326.72.1">326.72, subdivision 1</a>. Unless the project is an emergency project, the notice shall be given to the commissioner at least five calendar days before the project begins. The notice shall contain the following information:</p>
        <p>(1) a brief description of the work to be performed;</p>
        <p>(2) the name of the contracting entity;</p>
        <p>(3) the location and address of the project work site;</p>
        <p>(4) the approximate duration of the project;</p>
        <p>(5) the approximate amount of the asbestos involved in the project;</p>
        <p>(6) the name of any project manager; and</p>
        <p>(7) other information required by the commissioner.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.326.74.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/303/#laws.0.5.0">1987 c 303 s 5</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/303/#laws.0.12.0">1993 c 303 s 12</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1997/0/205/#laws.0.35.0">1997 c 205 s 35</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.326.75">
        <h1 class="shn">326.75 FEES.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.75.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.75.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Licensing fee.</span></h2>
          <p>A person required to be licensed under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.72">326.72 </a>shall, before receipt of the license and before causing asbestos-related work to be performed, pay the commissioner an annual license fee of $105.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.75.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.75.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Certification fee.</span></h2>
          <p>An individual required to be certified as an asbestos worker or asbestos site supervisor under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.73#stat.326.73.1">326.73, subdivision 1</a>, shall pay the commissioner a certification fee of $52.50 before the issuance of the certificate. An individual required to be certified as an asbestos inspector, asbestos management planner, or asbestos project designer under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.73">326.73, subdivisions 2, 3, and 4</a>, shall pay the commissioner a certification fee of $105 before the issuance of the certificate.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.75.3">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.75.3">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Permit fee.</span></h2>
          <p>Five calendar days before beginning asbestos-related work, a person shall pay a project permit fee to the commissioner equal to two percent of the total costs of the asbestos-related work. For asbestos-related work performed in single or multifamily residences, of greater than ten but less than 260 linear feet of asbestos-containing material on pipes, or greater than six but less than 160 square feet of asbestos-containing material on other facility components, a person shall pay a project permit fee of $35 to the commissioner.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.75.3a">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3a." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.75.3a">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3a.<span class="headnote">Asbestos-related training course permit fee.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner shall establish by rule a permit fee to be paid by a training course provider on application for a training course permit or renewal of a permit of each asbestos-related training course required for certification or registration.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.75.4">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.75.4">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Deposit of fees.</span></h2>
          <p>Fees collected under this section shall be deposited in the state government special revenue fund.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.326.75.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/303/#laws.0.6.0">1987 c 303 s 6</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1990/0/594/#laws.3.13.0">1990 c 594 art 3 s 13</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/303/#laws.0.13.0">1993 c 303 s 13</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/1/1/#laws.9.73.0">1Sp1993 c 1 art 9 s 73</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1994/0/567/#laws.0.20.0">1994 c 567 s 20</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/0/165/#laws.0.13.0">1995 c 165 s 13</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2021/0/30/#laws.3.45.0">2021 c 30 art 3 s 45</a>-47</p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.326.76">
        <h1 class="shn">326.76 DUTIES OF CONTRACTING ENTITIES.</h1>
        <p>A contracting entity intending to have asbestos-related work or asbestos management activity performed for its benefit shall include in the specifications and contracts for the work a requirement that the work be performed by contractors and subcontractors licensed or certified by the commissioner under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.70">326.70</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.81">326.81</a> and in accordance with rules prescribed by the commissioner related to asbestos-related work and asbestos management activity. No contracting entity shall allow asbestos-related work or asbestos management activity to be performed for its benefit unless it has seen that the person has a valid license or certificate. A contracting entity's failure to comply with this section does not relieve a person from any responsibilities under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.70">326.70</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.81">326.81</a>.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.326.76.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/303/#laws.0.7.0">1987 c 303 s 7</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1990/0/594/#laws.3.16.0">1990 c 594 art 3 s 16</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/303/#laws.0.14.0">1993 c 303 s 14</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1997/0/205/#laws.0.36.0">1997 c 205 s 36</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.326.77">
        <h1 class="shn">326.77 INDOOR AIR STANDARD.</h1>
        <p>(a) The commissioner may adopt rules establishing an indoor air standard for asbestos.</p>
        <p>(b) Until the rules become effective, asbestos remaining in the air following the completion of an abatement project shall not exceed .01 fibers greater than five microns in length per cubic centimeter of air.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.326.77.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/303/#laws.0.8.0">1987 c 303 s 8</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.326.78">
        <h1 class="shn">326.78 DUTIES OF COMMISSIONER.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.78.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.78.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Rulemaking.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner shall adopt and begin enforcement of rules necessary to implement sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.70">326.70</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.81">326.81</a>. The rules adopted shall not be duplicative of rules adopted by the commissioner of the Department of Labor and Industry. The rules shall include rules in the following areas:</p>
          <p>(1) application, enclosure, removal, and encapsulation procedures;</p>
          <p>(2) license and certificate qualification requirements;</p>
          <p>(3) examinations for obtaining a license and certificate;</p>
          <p>(4) training necessary for individual certification;</p>
          <p>(5) qualifications for managers of asbestos-related work projects;</p>
          <p>(6) asbestos-related work and asbestos management activity specifications;</p>
          <p>(7) any contractor bonding and insurance requirements deemed necessary by the commissioner;</p>
          <p>(8) license and certificate issuance and revocation procedures;</p>
          <p>(9) suspension or revocation of licenses or certificates;</p>
          <p>(10) license and certificate suspension and revocation criteria;</p>
          <p>(11) cleanup standards;</p>
          <p>(12) continuing education requirements; and</p>
          <p>(13) other rules necessary to implement sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.70">326.70</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.81">326.81</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.78.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.78.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Issuance of licenses and certificates.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner may issue licenses to persons and certificates to individuals who meet the criteria in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.70">326.70</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.81">326.81</a> and the commissioner's rules. Licenses shall be valid for 12 months. Certificates shall be valid for 12 months after the completion date on the approved training course diploma.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.78.3">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.78.3">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Delegation.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner may, in writing, delegate the inspection and enforcement authority granted in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.70">326.70</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.81">326.81</a> to other state agencies regulating asbestos.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.78.4">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.78.4">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/206/#laws.0.25.0">1993 c 206 s 25</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.78.5">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 5." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.78.5">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Subpoenas.</span></h2>
          <p>In matters under investigation by or pending before the commissioner under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.70">326.70</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.81">326.81</a>, the commissioner may issue subpoenas and compel the attendance of witnesses and the production of papers, books, records, documents, and other relevant evidentiary material. A person failing or refusing to comply with the subpoena or order may, upon application by the commissioner to the district court in any district, be ordered by the court to comply with the order or subpoena. The commissioner may also administer oaths and affirmations to witnesses. Depositions may be taken within or without the state in the manner provided by law for the taking of depositions in civil actions. A subpoena or other process or paper may be served upon any person anywhere within the state by an officer authorized to serve subpoenas in civil actions, with the same fees and mileage costs paid, and in the manner as prescribed by law, for process of the state district courts. Fees and mileage and other costs of persons subpoenaed by the commissioner shall be paid in the manner prescribed for proceedings in district court.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.78.6">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 6." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.78.6">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/206/#laws.0.25.0">1993 c 206 s 25</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.78.7">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 7." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.78.7">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/206/#laws.0.25.0">1993 c 206 s 25</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.78.8">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 8." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.78.8">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/206/#laws.0.25.0">1993 c 206 s 25</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.326.78.9">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 9." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/326/full#stat.326.78.9">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 9.<span class="headnote">Penalties.</span></h2>
          <p>A person who violates any of the requirements of sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.70">326.70</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.81">326.81</a> or any requirement, rule, or order issued under those sections is subject to a civil penalty of not more than $10,000 per day of violation. Penalties may be recovered in a civil action in the name of the state brought by the attorney general.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.326.78.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/303/#laws.0.9.0">1987 c 303 s 9</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/282/#laws.2.183.0">1989 c 282 art 2 s 183</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1990/0/594/#laws.3.16.0">1990 c 594 art 3 s 16</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/303/#laws.0.15.0">1993 c 303 s 15</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1994/0/465/#laws.3.70.0">1994 c 465 art 3 s 70</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/0/165/#laws.0.14.0">1995 c 165 s 14</a>,15; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/0/186/#laws.0.119.0">1995 c 186 s 119</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1997/0/205/#laws.0.37.0">1997 c 205 s 37</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.326.785">
        <h1 class="shn">326.785 ASBESTOS CONTAINMENT BARRIERS.</h1>
        <p>Notwithstanding Minnesota Rules, part <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/rules/4620.3568">4620.3568</a>, subparts 1 to 4, containment barriers, in the case of tunnel abatement enclosures, are limited to double critical barriers.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.326.785.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1990/0/381/#laws.0.1.0">1990 c 381 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/303/#laws.0.16.0">1993 c 303 s 16</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1997/0/205/#laws.0.38.0">1997 c 205 s 38</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.326.79"><b>326.79</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/206/#laws.0.25.0">1993 c 206 s 25</a>]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.326.80"><b>326.80</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/206/#laws.0.25.0">1993 c 206 s 25</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.326.81">
        <h1 class="shn">326.81 DISCRIMINATION; SANCTIONS.</h1>
        <p>A person who discriminates against or otherwise sanctions an employee who complains to or cooperates with the commissioner in administering sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.70">326.70</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/326.81">326.81</a> is guilty of a misdemeanor.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.326.81.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/303/#laws.0.12.0">1987 c 303 s 12</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1990/0/594/#laws.3.16.0">1990 c 594 art 3 s 16</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/303/#laws.0.19.0">1993 c 303 s 19</a></p>
      </div>
